# The Light of Shambhu’s Astrology 15
<note>original title: "Shambhu-hora-prakasha". <term>Shambhu</term> is a name for the deity Shiva; <term>Hora</term> refers to the branch of astrology (Jyotisha) dealing with hours and horoscopes; and <term>Prakasha</term> means "light" or "illumination," commonly used in titles for scholarly commentaries or treatises.</note>

--- Page 4 ---
<meta>This page marks the beginning of the Shuddhi Kaumudi, a treatise on Jyotisha (astrology). It opens with traditional invocations to Ganesha and Shiva, followed by an introduction to the lineage of the science and the qualifications required of an astrologer.</meta>

<center># Shuddhi Kaumudi</center>

<margin>
Shu.
Kau.
<note>Abbreviation for the title "Shuddhi Kaumudi" (The Moonlight of Purification/Correction).</note>
</margin>

Om! Auspiciousness! Salutations to the illustrious Ganesha. Om! I bow my head to the blissful Supreme Shiva, joined with the daughter of the mountain <note>Parvati</note>, who grants the desires of his devotees; who is the pure source of the gods' thoughts and provides every success. I bow to the Guru who possesses the face of the Lord of Elephants <note>Ganesha</note> and who, like a mother, provides support and light; I bow to the illustrious Guru named Matanga. 

This science was proclaimed by the illustrious Garga, Vasistha, Shaunaka, Shuka, Vyasa, and others, through the dialogue between Shiva and Parvati which removes all obstacles. This work, the *Varaha-dashaka-mani* <note>original: "Varahadashakamani", likely referring to the works of the astronomer-astrologer Varahamihira</note>, is expanded by its own majesty and wealth. Taking the essence of those works, the illustrious Khanjaraja, through his own intellect, now composes this work in gentle words. 3. 

Khanjaraja, for the playful delight of the wise and for the benefit of students, creates this essence of horoscopy <term>Hora: the branch of astrology dealing with individual birth charts and timing</term> called the "Illumination of Day and Night" <note>original: "Ahoratra Prakasham"</note> based on the teachings of the masters of horoscopy. 4.

**The Praise of the Science of Astrology**

This science is four-fold in its application, yet when understood through proper method, it is known as the "Three Branches" <term>Skandha Traya: the three divisions of Indian astrology consisting of Ganita (mathematics), Hora (horoscopy), and Samhita (mundane/practical astrology)</term>. All of this was spoken clearly by me, following the teachings of the Western/Roman schools <note>original: "Romake", referring to the Romaka Siddhanta which shows Greco-Roman influence on Indian astronomy</note>, which is supreme and should be seen by all. 5. 

The first system is the *Brahma-Siddhanta*, then the *Soma-Siddhanta*; the seventh science spoken of is the auspicious one. Others were placed by the wise: the *Brahma*, the *Surya* <note>The Sun-based system</note>, the *Soma* being the third. 6. The *Vasistha*, the *Paulisha*, the *Romasha*, and the *Parashara*—these are the seven proclaimed systems <term>Siddhanta: comprehensive astronomical treatises that form the basis for calendar making and predictions</term>, appearing in various forms both manifest and unmanifest.

**The Characteristics of an Astrologer**

One who is truly capable of crossing the ocean of the science of horoscopy; who is proficient in horoscopy, mathematics <term>Ganita</term>, and algebra <term>Bija-ganita</term>; whose mind is fixed on dharma and destiny; who can explain the clear proofs of the astronomical systems through various methods; who is skilled in the geometry of the celestial sphere <term>Gola</term>—such a person is fit to give predictions. 8. 

Just as food is not enjoyable without <unclear>salt/blessing</unclear>, and just as a king's kingdom does not shine without a learned priest, so too does a kingdom not shine at all without the presence of <insert>an astrologer</insert>...

<summary>This opening page introduces the Shuddhi Kaumudi, a work on astrology by Khanjaraja. It outlines the historical lineage of the science from sages like Vasistha and Varahamihira and defines the "Three Branches" of astrology while listing the seven foundational astronomical systems (Siddhantas).</summary>

<keywords>Shuddhi Kaumudi, Jyotisha, Ganesha, Shiva, Khanjaraja, Hora, Skandha Traya, Siddhanta, Surya Siddhanta, Daivajna, astrologer qualifications, Indian astronomy</keywords>

--- Page 5 ---
<meta>This page continues the introductory portion of the Shuddhi Kaumudi, transitioning from the praise of the science of astrology to the specific requirements of its practitioners and the foundational correspondences between the heavens and the human body.</meta>

One who is not deficient <note>meaning physically whole or without disability, according to traditional requirements for ritualists</note> and is an expert in the science of the spheres <note>original: "Golavid", referring to spherical astronomy and the movement of celestial bodies</note>, 9. who is a knower of the "Three Branches" <term>Skandhajna: a master of the three divisions of astrology—Siddhanta (astronomy), Samhita (omens/mundane astrology), and Hora (horoscopy)</term>, is pleasing in appearance, and is tranquil. Such a person is devoted to the worship of the Vedas and tradition <note>original: "shrautasmatta", referring to Shruti (revelation) and Smriti (tradition)</note>, possesses a firm mind, is free from pride, is a speaker of truth, and is cheerful. He alone is known as a true Astrologer <term>Daivajna: literally "one who knows the divine/fate"</term>, and none other. 10.

**The Characteristics of an Astrologer**

<note>This section describes various planetary associations and deity correspondences, though the transcription contains several technical abbreviations.</note>
In the movement of the winds... the lord of the vessel... Mars... the breath of life is Mercury... the conjunction of the seven... the power and lordship... the lord of Mercury... the son of Diti <note>referring to the Asuras or specific planetary influences</note>; Saturn is favorable through victory, providing success and the fulfillment of desires; Ketu... 11. 

Through the influence of the Sun and the host of wandering celestial bodies, the signs beginning with Aries are covered. These signs are governed by Ganesha, Brahma, Lakshmi, and the Earth-bearer <note>Vishnu</note>; the Heavy-Teacher <note>Jupiter</note>, the Vasus, Indra, and the Seven Sages. For the sake of understanding, this scroll is written by me. 12.

**The Benefit of the Birth Chart**

Actions are not usually successful without the horoscope <term>Janmapattri: a birth chart or "life-scroll"</term>, which increases fortune, prosperity, and splendor. When a birth chart is written for a person, it brings about various types of wealth and illuminates the path through the night of life. 13.
It has been said that the fruits of actions earned by humans in previous lives are revealed through this path, as the planetary periods <term>Dasha: the chronological stages of a person's life ruled by specific planets</term> unfold in their proper sequence. 14.

**The Anatomical Correspondence of the Zodiac**

The head, the face, the pair of arms, the heart, the stomach, the waist, the pelvis, the genitals, the thighs, the knees, the shanks, and the two feet are, in sequence, assigned to the zodiac signs starting from Aries. 15. This was declared by the ancients for the sake of clear understanding, as these signs represent the various limbs in their entirety without exception. 15.

**The Rulers and Synonyms of the Signs**

Mars, Venus, Mercury, the Moon, the Sun, Mercury, Venus, Mars, Jupiter, Saturn, Saturn, and Jupiter <note>This list follows the traditional sequence of planetary lords for the twelve signs</note>. 16.
Aries is also called *Kriya*; Taurus is known as *Vrishabha*; Gemini is called *Mithuna* or "The Pair"; 17. Cancer is known as *Karka* or *Kulira*; the sign of Leo is called *Simha* or *Leyo*; the sign of the Maiden <note>Virgo</note> is called *Kanya* or *Pathona*. 18. Then follows the sign of the Scales...

# The Definitions and Synonyms of the Zodiac Signs

...the end of the sign Aquarius <note>original: "Ghatontakas", referring to the transition from the Water-bearer</note>, and the fourth from Libra; the sign <term>Vrischika: Scorpio</term> is also known as the "Insect," the "Scorpion," and the "Reptile" <note>original: "Sarisripa"</note> due to its nature. 18. Half-man and half-beast is <term>Dhanu: Sagittarius</term>, also called the "Archer," the "Bow-holder," and the "Armed One," as described by the ancient masters of the science of time <note>original: "Yavana", often referring to the Ionian/Greek influence in Indian astrology</note>. 21. <term>Makara: Capricorn</term> is the "Sea-monster," the "Fish-crocodile," or simply the "Crocodile." <term>Kumbha: Aquarius</term> is the "Pot," the "Water-bearer," the "Vessel," and the "Pitcher." 21. <term>Meena: Pisces</term> is known as the "Fish," the "Two-fishes," the "Scaly One," and the "Whale." These are the names of the signs established by the ancient Sages. 22.

### The Physical Forms of the Signs

The sign of Pisces is shown as two fishes; <term>Mithuna: Gemini</term> is a human couple—a man and a woman—holding a lute and a club <note>The lute symbolizes the arts, and the club represents protection or strength</note>. <term>Tula: Libra</term> is a man holding scales. 23. <term>Kanya: Virgo</term> is a maiden standing in a boat, carrying grain and a lamp in her hands. <term>Kumbha: Aquarius</term> is a man carrying an empty water jar on his shoulder. 24.

### Colors and Elemental Qualities

The colors of the signs are respectively: red <note>Aries</note>, white <note>Taurus</note>, parrot-green <note>Gemini</note>, pale-red <note>Cancer</note>, yellowish-white <note>Leo</note>, variegated <note>Virgo</note>, black <note>Libra</note>, golden-brown <note>Scorpio</note>, tawny <note>Sagittarius</note>, <unclear>grey</unclear> <note>Capricorn</note>, deep brown <note>Aquarius</note>, and the color of the sea <note>Pisces</note>. These are the colors associated with their inherent natures. 25.

### Classifications of the Signs

The signs are further categorized as:
*   **Movable** <term>Chara</term>, **Fixed** <term>Sthira</term>, and **Dual-natured** <term>Dvisvabhava</term>.
*   **Cruel** <term>Asaumya</term> and **Gentle** <term>Saumya</term>.
*   **Odd** and **Even**.
These classifications apply to the signs beginning with Aries <note>original: "Kriya", a synonym for Mesha/Aries</note> in sequence. 26.

Aries, Leo, and Sagittarius are the <term>Kshatriyas: the warrior and ruler class</term>. Taurus, Virgo, and Capricorn are the <term>Vaishyas: the merchant class</term>. Gemini, Libra, and Aquarius are the <term>Shudras: the service class</term>. Cancer, Scorpio, and Pisces are the <term>Brahmins: the priestly and scholarly class</term>. 27.

### Physical Traits and Temporal Strength

The first half of Sagittarius and the second half of Capricorn are considered human-like. Taurus, Leo, and Aries are four-footed <note>quadrupeds</note>. Virgo, Gemini, Libra, and the first half of Sagittarius are two-footed <note>bipeds or humans</note>. 28. Cancer and Pisces are water-dwelling creatures. Scorpio is a multi-footed creature. Human signs are strong during the day, while the animal and water signs possess strength in the evening and at night. 29. 

Signs that rise with the head first <note>original: "Shirshodaya"</note> are generally auspicious, while those that rise tail-first <note>original: "Prishtodaya"</note> or both ways have different strengths based on their position in the sky at the time of birth. 30.

<meta>This page continues the foundational definitions of astrology, transitioning from the physical descriptions of the zodiac signs to their technical classifications, the twelve houses of a horoscope, and the system of divisional charts known as the Seven Vargas.</meta>

<margin>
31
</margin>
Half of the zodiac is said to be the eastern half, and the other half is the western. The degrees of the <term>Bhacakra: Zodiac Wheel</term> are measured from the rising sign <note>The "Lagna" or Ascendant</note> for the first half, and the remaining portions are considered the "consumable" or passing degrees for the western half. 31.

### The Classification of Signs by Day and Night

The signs of the celestial wheel are as follows: **Aries**, **Taurus**, **Gemini**, **Cancer**, **Leo**, and **Virgo** are known as nocturnal signs <note>original: "Nishabala", signs that gain strength at night</note>. **Libra**, **Sagittarius**, **Scorpio**, **Aquarius**, **Capricorn**, and **Pisces** are called diurnal signs <note>original: "Divatmaka", signs that are powerful during the day</note>. 32-33.

### Manner of Rising

**Aries**, **Taurus**, and **Cancer**—along with **Sagittarius** and **Capricorn**—are known as signs that rise from the rear <note>original: "Prishthodaya". These signs are thought to emerge tail-first over the horizon</note>. **Gemini**, **Leo**, **Virgo**, **Libra**, **Scorpio**, and **Aquarius** are called "Head-rising" signs <note>original: "Shirshodaya", considered more auspicious for certain results</note>. **Pisces** is said to rise with both sides together. 34.

### The Strength of the Signs

A zodiac sign is truly strong when it is aspected by or joined with its own planetary ruler, or by the planets **Mercury** <note>original: "Budha"</note> or **Jupiter** <note>original: "Vachaspati"</note>. If it is not aspected by these, its strength is diminished. 35. A sign yields its fruits according to its own nature, but its effects are modified when it is occupied or aspected by benefic or malefic planets. 36.

### The Twelve Houses of the Horoscope

Whichever house <note>original: "Bhava"</note> is joined with or aspected by benefic planets will see increase and prosperity; conversely, those influenced by malefic planets will suffer loss or destruction. 37. The twelve houses, known in the science of <term>Horika: Astrology</term>, must be contemplated in this order:
1. **The Body** <note>The Self or Physical form</note>
2. **Wealth**
3. **Siblings** <note>Co-borns</note>
4. **Home/Happiness**
5. **Children** <note>Intellect and offspring</note>
6. **Enemies** <note>Debts and diseases</note>
7. **Wife** <note>Partnerships</note>
8. **Death** <note>Longevity</note>
9. **Dharma** <note>Fortune and religion</note>
10. **Action** <note>Career/Karma</note>
11. **Gains**
12. **Loss** <note>Expenditure</note> 38.

### The Seven Divisional Charts (Saptavarga)

The ancient masters have described the seven divisions of a sign used to determine planetary strength. These are the **Sign** itself, the **Hora** <note>a half-division of 15 degrees</note>, the **Drekkana** <note>a third-part or decan</note>, the **Saptamsha** <note>a seventh-part</note>, the **Navamsha** <note>a ninth-part</note>, the **Dwadashamsha** <note>a twelfth-part</note>, and the **Trimshamsha** <note>a thirtieth-part</note>. 39.

In an odd sign, the first half belongs to the **Sun** and the second to the **Moon**; in an even sign, this order is reversed. The **Drekkanas** belong to the lords of the 1st, 5th, and 9th signs from the sign in question. 40.

The **Navamsha** divisions for **Aries**, **Leo**, and **Sagittarius** begin with Aries; for **Taurus**, **Virgo**, and **Capricorn**, they begin with Capricorn; for **Gemini**, **Libra**, and **Aquarius**, they begin with Libra; and for **Cancer**, **Scorpio**, and **Pisces**, they begin with Cancer. 41.

The lords of the **Trimshamshas** <note>the 30 degrees of a sign</note> for odd signs are, in order: **Mars**, **Saturn**, **Jupiter**, **Mercury**, and **Venus**. For even signs, the experts in nativities <note>original: "Jatakajña"</note> declare the order is reversed. 42.

Now, concerning the **Kendras** <note>the angular or "central" houses: 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th</note>...

### The Classification of Houses

The four **Angles** <note>original: "Catushtaya-Kendra"</note> are the primary houses. Among these, the first is the residence of all things. Next are the houses of **Wealth** and **Gain** <note>Houses 2 and 11</note>. The **Cadent** houses <note>original: "Apoklima"; the 3rd, 6th, 9th, and 12th houses</note> follow the Succedent houses. 43. The **Root-Trine** <note>original: "Mulatrikona"</note> includes the ninth and fifth houses. The sixth house is also known as a trine of enemies <note>original: "Ripubha"</note>. 44.

The houses of **Growth** <note>original: "Upacaya"; houses 3, 6, 10, and 11</note> are said by the wise to be the third, sixth, tenth, and eleventh. The term **Best Division** <note>original: "Vargottama"; refers to a planet occupying the same sign in both the main chart and the divisional Ninth-chart</note> is used by the learned to describe specific auspicious placements. 45.

### Synonyms for the Twelve Houses

For the **First House** <note>original: "Lagna"</note>, the names used by the wise are: **Form**, **Body**, **The Beginning**, **Physicality**, **Limb**, and **The Rising Sign**. For the **Second House**, the names are: **Self**, **Treasury**, **Wealth**, and **Assets**. 46.

The **Fourth House** is called: **The Underworld** <note>original: "Patala" and "Hibuka"</note>, **The Chest**, **Water**, and **Cloud**. These are the names given to the fourth house by the tradition. 47.

Regarding the **Third House**, the names are: **Valor**, **Prowess**, and **Brotherhood**. For the **Eighth House**, the names are: **Destruction**, **Longevity**, **Life-span**, and **The Vulnerable Point** <note>original: "Chidra"</note>; it is also called the **Place of Dissolution**. 48.

The **Tenth House**, located at the zenith of the zodiac, is known as: **Mid-day Action**, **Rank**, **The Native’s Kingdom**, **The Sky**, **The Atmosphere**, and **The Heavens**. 49.

The **Fifth House** is known as: **Knowledge**, **Progeny**, **The Machine**, **Sacred Mantras**, and **Service**. The **Ninth House** is called: **Penance**, **Fortune**, and **The Law**. 50.

The **Seventh House** has been called by ancient teachers: **The Setting**, **Desire**, **The Path**, **The Lost**, **Wife**, and **Passion**. 51.

The **Sixth House** is known as: **Enemy** and **Opponent**. The **Eleventh House** is remembered as: **Income**, **Gain**, and **Acquisition**. 52.

-> Thus concludes the first chapter, titled "The Classification of the Signs," in the *Horaprakasha* composed by the wise King Shiva for the enlightenment of those seeking knowledge. 52. <-

### Chapter One Concluded
**Thus ends the first chapter on the Classification of Signs in the Illustrious Shiva’s *Horaprakasha*.**

<meta>This page transitions from the definitions of the twelve houses to the specific characteristics, roles, and elemental natures of the planets (Grahas). It establishes the celestial hierarchy, comparing the planetary system to a royal court.</meta>

### The Planetary Cabinet and Their Roles

The Sun is the **King** <note>original: "Nrupa"</note>; the Moon is also of royal status. Mars is the **Leader of the Army** <note>original: "Sainya-neta"</note>. Mercury is known as the **Prince** <note>original: "Kumara"</note>. The wise ministers are Jupiter and Venus. Saturn, the son of the Sun, is designated as the **Servant** <note>original: "Sevaka"</note>. 1-2.

### Physical Appearances and Deities

The colors of the planets are as follows: the Sun is copper-red; the Moon is white; Mars is deep red; Mercury is green like the blade of a durva grass; Jupiter is yellow; Venus is variegated/bright white; and Saturn is black. 4.

The presiding deities associated with the planets starting from the Sun are: **Fire** <note>Agni</note>, **Water** <note>Varuna</note>, **Skanda** <note>the god of war</note>, **Vishnu**, **Indra**, **Indrani** <note>Sachi</note>, and **Brahma** <note>Virinchi</note>. 4.

### The Lords of the Directions

The lords of the directions and the planets are stated in order: Jupiter, the Sun, the Moon, Mars, Mercury, and the others. They govern the various quarters of the compass. 5.

### Natural Benefics and Malefics

The **Cruel** <note>original: "Krura" or "Papa" (malefic)</note> planets are the Sun, Saturn, and Mars. Mercury is considered malefic when joined with other malefic planets. The Moon is malefic when it is **waning** <note>original: "kshina"</note>. The **Gentle** <note>original: "Saumya" (benefic)</note> planets are Jupiter, Venus, the full Moon, and Mercury when it is not associated with malefics. These are considered strong when they possess their natural power. 6.

### Social Orders and Castes

Jupiter and Venus are the **Priests** <note>original: "Vipra" (Brahmins)</note>. Mars and the Sun are the **Warriors** <note>original: "Rajanya" (Kshatriyas)</note>. The Moon and Mercury represent the **Traders** <note>original: "Visha" (Vaishyas)</note>. Saturn is the lord of the **Laborers** <note>original: "Shudra"</note>. Among those of mixed or low birth, Saturn is also the ruler. 7.

### Genders and Primary Qualities

The male planets are the Sun, Mars, and Jupiter. The female planets are the Moon and Venus. Mercury and Saturn are considered **neuter** <note>original: "shastha," often referring to eunuchs in this context</note>. 8.

The primary qualities <note>Gunas</note> are assigned thus: the Sun, Moon, and Jupiter are **Luminous/Pure** <note>Sattva</note>; Venus and Mercury are **Active/Passionate** <note>Rajas</note>; and Mars and Saturn are **Dark/Inert** <note>Tamas</note>. 8.

### The Seven Tastes

The tastes associated with the planets are:
*   **Pungent**: The Sun
*   **Salt**: The Moon
*   **Bitter**: Mars
*   **Mixed**: Mercury
*   **Sweet**: Jupiter
*   **Sour**: Venus
*   **Astringent**: Saturn 9.

### Time Periods and Realms of Existence

The planets govern various units of time: half-years, moments, days, seasons, months, and fortnights. 10.

Regarding the realms of the afterlife: Venus and the Moon lead to the **Realm of the Ancestors** <note>original: "Pitri-loka"</note>. Jupiter represents **Heaven** <note>original: "Svarga"</note>. The Sun and Mars lead to the world of mortals, while Saturn and Mercury lead to the **Lower Realms** <note>original: "Naraka" (Purgatory/Hell)</note>. 11-12.

The zodiac signs—Aries, Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Leo, Virgo, Libra, Scorpio, Sagittarius, Capricorn, Aquarius, and Pisces—follow in their respective order... <note>The text breaks off here as it begins discussing exaltation points.</note>

### Primary Trines and Planetary Portions
<note>This section defines the specific degrees within signs where planets are exceptionally strong, a concept known as Moolatrikona.</note>

The portions are as follows: Thirteen, ten, three, twenty-eight, fifteen, five, and twenty. In the sign of **Leo**, the Sun’s power zone extends to twenty degrees. In **Taurus**, the Moon occupies the trine after the first three degrees. For **Aries**, the first twelve degrees belong to Mars. In **Virgo**, the trine of Mercury is at fifteen degrees. For **Sagittarius**, the first ten degrees are Jupiter's zone. In **Libra**, the first five degrees are for Venus, and for **Aquarius**, the first twenty degrees belong to Saturn. The remaining portions of these signs are considered the planets' own houses.

### The Natural Relationships of the Planets
<note>In Jyotisha, planets are not isolated; they have inherent "social" relationships—friendship, neutrality, or enmity—which affect how they interpret a birth chart.</note>

The natural relationships as taught by the <term>Jataka-shastra-vidbhih</term> <note>original: "those who know the science of birth-horoscopy"</note> are summarized here:

| Planet | Friends | Neutral | Enemies |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Sun** | Moon, Mars, Jupiter | Mercury | Venus, Saturn |
| **Moon** | Sun, Mercury | Mars, Jupiter, Venus, Saturn | None |
| **Mars** | Sun, Moon, Jupiter | Venus, Saturn | Mercury |
| **Mercury** | Sun, Venus | Mars, Jupiter, Saturn | Moon |
| **Jupiter** | Sun, Moon, Mars | Saturn | Mercury, Venus |
| **Venus** | Mercury, Saturn | Mars, Jupiter | Sun, Moon |
| **Saturn** | Mercury, Venus | Jupiter | Sun, Moon, Mars |

### Temporary and Compound Relationships
The <term>Tatkalika</term> <note>temporary or situational</note> friendships are determined by the current positions of the planets in the sky. If planets are located in the 2nd, 3rd, 4th, 10th, 11th, or 12th houses from one another, they act as temporary friends. Otherwise, they are temporary enemies. By combining the natural (<term>Nisarga</term>) and temporary relationships, a wise astrologer determines the five-fold bond: Great Friend, Friend, Neutral, Enemy, and Great Enemy. 23-24.

### Strength through Direction and Time
Planets also gain strength based on the direction they face and the time of day:

*   **Directional Strength** <note>Dig-bala</note>: Mercury and Jupiter are strong in the **East** (the Ascendant). The Sun and Mars are strong in the **South** (the 10th house). Saturn is strong in the **West** (the 7th house). The Moon and Venus are strong in the **North** (the 4th house). 25.
*   **Temporal Strength**: Jupiter is always strong. Mercury is strong at the **start of the day**. The Sun and Mars are strong at **midday**. Saturn is strong at the **end of the day**. The Moon and Venus are strong at **midnight** and the end of the night. 26.

<summary>This page details the mathematical and relational rules of Vedic astrology, specifically the Moolatrikona degrees, the permanent friendships between planets, and how they gain strength based on their directional and temporal placement.</summary>
<keywords>Moolatrikona, Planetary Friendship, Dig-bala, Tatkalika Maitri, Jyotisha, Astrological Strength</keywords>

--- Page 11 ---
<meta>This page continues the technical classification of planetary strengths, specifically focusing on "Directional Strength" (Dik-bala), "Temporal Strength" (Kala-bala), and the physical characteristics (Rupa) used to identify planetary influences in a person's appearance.</meta>

### Directional Strength of the Planets
<note>This section describes *Dik-bala*, the strength a planet gains by being positioned in a specific compass direction within the birth chart.</note>

Naturally stronger is the one who possesses directional strength. In the **Ascendant** <note>the 1st House, representing the East</note>, **Jupiter**, the **Moon**, and **Venus** are powerful. **Mars** and the **Sun** reach their full strength in the 10th House <note>the Midheaven, representing the South</note>. **Mercury** and the **Sun** <note>original: "Ravijno"</note> are also considered strong there. This is what the wise call "Directional Strength" <term>Dik-bala</term>.

<margin>28</margin> **Venus** and the **Moon** are endowed with vigor when they occupy feminine signs <note>Taurus, Cancer, Virgo, Scorpio, Capricorn, and Pisces</note>. In masculine signs, the other planets are considered strong. When the lords of these signs are themselves powerful and auspicious, the planets they host also flourish; otherwise, they are considered weak or "failing."

### Planetary Motion and Retrogression
<note>This refers to the visual phenomenon where a planet appears to move backward in the sky, known as "Vakra" or retrograde motion.</note>

When **Mars** <note>the "Red Planet"</note> is retrograde or moving in the northern declination, it is said to possess great strength. In planetary conjunctions <note>when two planets appear very close together</note>, if they are in the same degree, their individual strengths must be weighed. 

### The Five Stages of Planetary Age
<note>The "Avasthas" or states describe the maturity of a planet's energy, often compared to the human life cycle.</note>

<margin>31</margin> The **Moon** is powerful during the night, and its strength is calculated based on its distance from the **Sun**. The benefic planets—**Mercury**, **Jupiter**, and **Venus**—are naturally strong in the bright half of the lunar month. 

Regarding their ages:
*   **Mars** is considered a **Youth**.
*   **Mercury** is considered an **Infant** or Child.
*   **Venus** and the **Moon** are considered **Middle-aged** or in their prime.
*   **Saturn**, **Jupiter**, and the **Sun** are regarded as **Old** or Elderly.

<margin>32</margin> A planet is also strong when it resides in its own division <term>Vargottama</term>, or when it is placed in its own sign, its trine, or a friendly sign. If it is joined by the **Moon** or an auspicious planet, its power is increased; if joined by a "cruel" <note>malefic</note> planet, it is diminished.

### The Appearance of the Deities
<note>Ancient Indian astrology assigns physical traits to each planet. These were used by physicians and astrologers to identify which planet dominated a person's constitution.</note>

**The Sun** is described as having a body that is dark-red, with eyes like honey. He is courageous and of a steady nature. 
**Jupiter** has honey-tawny eyes <note>original: "Madhu-pinga-netra"</note> and a large, golden-hued body; he is wise and possesses a deep, resonant voice. 

<margin>2</margin> **Venus** is joyful and possesses a beautiful body and lovely eyes. **Mars** is fierce, with a youthful but powerful frame, and a reddish complexion. **Saturn** is lean and tall, with dark hair, and is associated with wind and phlegm <note>the humors of the body</note>. The **Moon** is wise and has a slender but beautiful form, reflecting the nature of its phase.

### The Ten States of the Planets
<note>This section defines the "Avasthas" or psychological/functional states of the planets based on their placement in the zodiac.</note>

42: **Blazing** <term>Deepta</term>, **Radiant** <term>Vidipta</term>, **Delighted** <term>Harshita</term>, **Peaceful** <term>Shanta</term>, **Powerful** <term>Shakta</term>, **Afflicted** <term>Peedita</term>, **Terrified** <term>Bhita</term>, **Mutilated** <term>Vaikalya</term>, **Miserable** <term>Deena</term>, and **Dormant** <term>Suptta</term>. These are the ten states of the planets <term>Khecharas</term> declared by me. 

43: A planet is **Blazing** when in its sign of exaltation; **Delighted** in the house of a friend; **Healthy** <note>original: "Swastha"</note> in its own sign; and **Tranquil** <note>original: "Prashanta"</note> in the divisional charts of gentle planets. 44: It is **Calm** when it is observed by auspicious planets. It is **Mutilated** <term>Vaikalya</term> when it is joined by cruel planets. It is **Miserable** when it occupies a sign of depression or debilitation. 45: It is **Afflicted** when defeated in planetary war; and **Powerless** <note>original: "Vivala"</note> when staying in an enemy's house.

Adorned with jewelry and good sense, they become the destroyer of groups of enemies. 

48: In the **Calm** <term>Shanta</term> state, one has a wife, friends, and sons. They are a benefactor to others, a doer of good deeds, and peaceful. They seek the meaning of the scriptures, may be a minister to a king, and live with joy and splendor. 49: In the **Powerful** <term>Shakta</term> state, one is capable in policy, a benefactor to others, and pleasing to their kinsmen. They become famous...

<margin>Shri</margin> <note>A sacred invocation for prosperity and success at the start of a section.</note>

The Sun and the Moon, when exceeding three signs in the zodiac, are observed to have specific strengths. The strength of Venus, Mars, and Saturn arises in a similar way. Sixty <note>sixty points or 'virupas' are the standard unit of planetary strength</note> is assigned to the Moon’s strength during the night hours. **14** 

In the divisions of eight and three, the lunar strength is calculated. During the night, the Moon and the Lord of the Night <note>the planetary ruler of the specific night</note> are considered. When Jupiter is in a three-part division, this strength is declared by the sages. **15**

In the Year, the Month, the Day, and the Hour (<term>Hora</term>), Venus and Mercury are said to possess strength if they are the rulers of those times. This is called the "Time-Based Strength" (*Kala-bala*), which is of four types as known by the experts of <term>Jatakashastra</term> <note>The classical science of natal astrology.</note>. **16**

### Calculation for Determining the Lord of the Year (<term>Varshapa</term>)

Now the method for determining the Lord of the Year is presented. From the given lifespan-quantity, add eight and multiply by the number of the Sun. Divide by the remainder and add four... <note>The text describes a complex mathematical algorithm for "Solar Return" charts</note> ...according to the <term>Grahalaghava</term> <note>A famous 16th-century astronomical treatise used to simplify complex planetary calculations.</note>, multiply the planetary positions by sixty-five and six. After dividing and adjusting for the signs, the Lord of the Year and Month is found. 

The positions of Mercury are always adjusted by two divisions. For the South, the Sun and Saturn are known to be together. The Sun and Venus's group are calculated similarly. This result is what becomes the <term>Ayanabala</term> <note>Strength derived from a planet's northern or southern declination from the celestial equator.</note>. **17**

The Sun’s strength is said to be derived from its friends. The combination of motional strength (*Cheshta-bala*) and declination strength (*Ayana-bala*) results in the "Subduing Strength" (*Vasha-virya*). **18**

The calculation of declination (<term>Kranti</term>) according to the **Grahalaghava** is as follows: Use the segments of the rulers of the days. By using the tropical <note>original: "Sayana", referring to the zodiac adjusted for the precession of the equinoxes</note> positions of the planets, calculate the elapsed degrees and the remainders. Multiply by ten and divide by the radius. This determines the direction (North or South) of the celestial globe. The average of the two results yields the strength. For Aries and the other signs, this constitutes the planetary power. **19**

The tropical Sun plus three signs becomes the "Center"...

### The Refinement of Potency

Now, the refinement of the planetary potency (<term>Virya</term>) for the lords of the signs is described. When a quarter of a planetary aspect (<term>Drishti</term> <note>The 'glance' or mathematical influence one planet casts upon another.</note>) is present, or when the three planets are devoid of aspect, the calculation of the "Six-fold Strength" (<term>Shadbala</term>) becomes clear and refined. **24** 

The seed of the strength of the twelve houses, beginning with the Body (<term>Tanvadi</term> <note>Referring to the 12 houses of a horoscope, starting with the 1st house or Ascendant.</note>), should be understood by men. ### On the Timing of Conception (<term>Nisheke</term>)

When the Sun and Venus are endowed with strength, they favor the birth of males; the Moon and Mars favor the birth of females. If Saturn aspects the seventh house from the Moon or the Ascendant, or if Mercury is in such a position, then a man may become sterile. **1** At the time of conception, if the Moon is in an "Upachaya" <note>The 3rd, 6th, 10th, or 11th houses, known as houses of growth.</note> sign and is aspected by Mars, it is favorable for women. If the Sun and Moon are in each other's signs or aspected by each other, the union of man and woman is successful. **2**

Regarding the moment of impregnation (<term>Nisheka</term>), one should examine the lord of the Ascendant and the lord of the sign. As the nature of the planets is, so shall the character of the man be during the union. If malefic planets are joined or aspecting, it brings anger and defects; if benefics, the person is free from such faults. **3** For women, the sixteen nights following the menses... <note>The text transitions into the biological-astrological rules for the timing of pregnancy.</note>

...[conception results] in even signs for men and odd signs for women; a male child is born through their union. **4** When Saturn and Venus are in their own divisions <note>original: "varga," referring to the sub-divisional charts like Navamsha</note>, and Jupiter is situated in a trine <note>The 5th or 9th houses, considered very auspicious.</note> or the Ascendant <note>original: "Lagna," the sign rising on the eastern horizon at the moment of birth or conception.</note> while endowed with strength, then the birth of an embryo occurs. **5** 

When Jupiter is in the Ascendant with strength, even if it resides in a trine in an enemy’s sign, for those who are without "seed" <note>referring to low fertility or impotence; original: "nishkala"</note>, the astrologer should observe the aspects <note>The mathematical "glance" or influence one planet has on another.</note> to determine if they are devoid of influence. **6** As is the nature of the mind and body at that moment, so shall be the creature in the womb. The son of the Sun <note>Saturn</note> grants his own fruit, while the son of the Moon <note>Mercury</note> gives various strengths and pleasures. **11** If Venus is in the seventh and Rahu <note>The North Node of the Moon, often associated with eclipses and karmic disturbances.</note> is in a water-sign, death may occur in the eighth year of life due to the nature of the planetary strength. **12** If the Moon and Mars are in the eighth, and the Sun is in a yellow <note>possibly referring to the color associated with certain zodiac signs or planetary rays</note> sign, it is said to cause great pain during the night. **13** 

The Sun and Venus are known as the Father and Mother <note>This refers to the primary significators (karakas) for parents in a daytime birth.</note>; however, it is said that Saturn and the Moon take the names of Father and Mother for those born at night. **14** 

If the Ascendant and the Moon are situated between malefic planets <note>original: "krura," meaning 'cruel' or 'harsh' planets like Mars or Saturn.</note> at the time of impregnation <term>Nisheka</term> <note>The ceremony or moment of conception.</note>, or are joined or aspected by them without the influence of benefics, the woman or the embryo will surely meet with death. **15** 

If the Ascendant or the Moon is influenced by malefics, or if Mars is in the eighth house, or when Saturn and the Sun are in specific positions, the woman may suffer a miscarriage or the child may be born with a crooked body. **16** 

When Venus and Saturn are aspected by Mercury, the child is given to the mother by the father’s line; if the Sun is in the house of action <note>the 10th house</note>, the mother’s health will be delicate. **17** When the Moon is joined by malefics, death is certain; but if influenced by benefics, it shall be auspicious. **18** 

If at the time of birth the Ascendant is in the middle of the seventh house, death will occur by the strike of a weapon <note>original: "shastra-prahara"</note>; of this, there is no doubt. **19** 

<summary>This page details the astrological conditions for successful conception, the determination of the child's sex, and malefic configurations that may lead to miscarriage or the death of the mother and infant.</summary>

...Through the continuous sequence of the months, the memory, power of agitation, and intellect are formed. **20** Venus, Mars, Jupiter, the Sun, Saturn, the Moon, and the Ascendant <note>original: "Lagna"</note> are the lords of the months. If these planets are afflicted by malefic influences <note>Harmful planets like Mars or Saturn that can cause distress in a chart.</note> or are weak, there is great suffering in those months. **21** 

If a planet is in its sign of debilitation <note>original: "Nichaga"; the position where a planet is at its weakest and most negative.</note>, or is combust <note>Too close to the sun to be seen.</note>, or is joined with malefic planets, a miscarriage <term>Garbhapata</term> <note>literally "falling of the womb"</note> occurs in the month ruled by that planet. **22** 

O Creator of Men! If Saturn and Mars are in the Ascendant of the woman at the time of conception, the pregnancy will be destroyed or result in a miscarriage. **23** If the Sun and Jupiter are in odd signs <note>Signs like Aries, Gemini, etc., considered masculine in nature.</note>, a male child is born. If the Moon, Venus, and Mars are in even signs <note>Signs like Taurus, Cancer, etc., considered feminine.</note>, the birth of a female child is predicted. **24** 

When Jupiter and the Sun are in even signs, or if Mercury is in a dual-natured sign <note>Signs like Gemini or Pisces that represent duality.</note>, twins are said to be born. Similarly, if Venus, Mercury, and the Moon are in feminine signs and are endowed with strength, twin girls will be born. **25** 

If the Sun is in an odd sign and the Moon is in an even sign, or if they are both aspected by Mars, or if the Moon and Mercury are in odd signs and Venus is in the Ascendant, then a person of the third gender <note>original: "Kliba"; referring to those who are neither typically male nor female, often translated as eunuch or hermaphrodite in historical contexts.</note> is born. **26** 

If the Sun and the Moon are in the Ascendant and look upon each other, or if they are in a mutual exchange of signs, and if they are joined by Saturn or Mercury, then a child is produced through the strength of Jupiter. **27** 

If the Moon is in the fifth, seventh, or ninth house from the Ascendant and is joined with malefic planets, while the auspicious planets are weak, the child will be born with physical deformities or with more or fewer than the usual number of limbs. **28** 

If Mars is in the sign of the Sun, or the Sun is in the sign of Mars, and they are joined with malefic planets in the Ascendant, while the Moon is in the house of weapons <note>The 6th or 8th houses, often associated with injury.</note>, the embryo will be entwined by a serpent <note>original: "sarpena samveshtita"; a traditional astrological metaphor for the umbilical cord being wrapped around the child.</note>. **29** 

When the malefic planets are strong and the auspicious ones are devoid of power at the time of conception, the woman will surely suffer. **30** If the Sun is in an odd sign and the Moon is in the sign of Capricorn or the Ascendant, and they are joined by malefic planets, the child will be a dwarf. If the lords of the fifth, ninth, and the Ascendant are weak, the child will be born without arms or legs. **31** 

If Mars is in the Ascendant or in the eighth house, and the Sun and Saturn are in the seventh, while Mercury is weak, the child will have defective teeth. If the Moon is in the eighth house and is aspected by malefics, the birth will be difficult. **32** 

Mars and other planets grant their own fruits as previously mentioned by the ancient sages. One should determine the good and bad fortune of the father and mother from the strength of the planets in the various houses. **33** If the auspicious planets are in the angles <note>original: "Kendra"; the 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th houses.</note> or trines...

The fetus at that time remains endowed with happiness and well-being, possessing its limbs and physical form. **34** If Jupiter <note>original: "Guru"</note> and Mercury <note>original: "Saumya"</note> are devoid of strength in the twelfth house <term>Vyaya</term> or in the trines <term>Trikona</term>, the child may be born with physical deficiencies or deformities in their limbs. **35** ...In these three conditions, if the Moon is afflicted at the time of conception <term>Nisheka</term>, death may occur, or the child may suffer greatly. **39** The various combinations of planetary strengths have been described; the wise understand the auspicious results of the benefic planets and the Moon, and they should carefully consider the mixed results arising from varied influences. **40**

# ## Thus ends the Chapter on Conception

If the planetary positions at the time of conception <term>Adhana</term> are unknown, the time of birth can be determined by the positions of the planets in the centers <term>Kendra</term> <note>The 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th houses of a birth chart, which are considered the pillars of a horoscope.</note>. When all planets are considered, the time of delivery <term>Sutika Kala</term> is explained by the wise. **1**

In the seventh house from the Ascendant, or in the sign occupied by the Moon <note>original: "Shashi"</note>, or in the fifth house, as agreed upon by the sages... **2** When the Moon <note>original: "Himago"</note> reaches the specific sign or degree related to the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term>, the birth of the child occurs. **3** When the planets are positioned in the fourth house <term>Rasatala</term>, the fetus at that time is said to be well-formed and happy. **4** Depending on the strength of the Moon at the time of rising, the signs and divisions determine the timing; some say the birth occurs when the Moon reaches the sign of the conception-ascendant. **5** The number of signs passed since the night of conception indicates the time that has elapsed, and from those signs, the determination of time <term>Kala-vinishchaya</term> should be made. **6** The birth occurs in the month indicated by whether the conception took place in a fixed sign <term>Sthira-griha</term> or a dual-natured sign <term>Ubhayarka</term> <note>Signs like Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, or Pisces</note>... **7**

When the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term> is situated in Taurus, Leo, Aries, or Scorpio...

If Saturn <note>original: "Suryasuta," literally "Son of the Sun"</note> is in the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term>, or if the Sun and Saturn are together, or if Mars is in a similar position, the infant will have its limbs wrapped in the umbilical cord <term>Nalaka veshtitanga</term>. **8** If two malefic planets are strong and positioned in the [seventh or eighth] houses and aspected by other malefics, the child will likewise be wrapped by the cord. **9** If Saturn and Mars <note>original: "Yamaro"</note> are in the portions of Mercury or Venus <note>original: "Saumya-shukra-amsha"</note>, they cause the same. If the Moon is in the Ascendant and Mars is in the eighth house, the child is surely born with the cord. **10** 

Furthermore, if a malefic is in the eighth house, it destroys [family members]: in the second house, it kills the brother; in the third, the sister; in the fourth, the mother; and in the fifth, it kills the child itself. **11** If birth occurs after many months <note>possibly referring to a late-term pregnancy</note>, the child may be born with teeth <note>original: "sadanta"</note>. Even then, if a malefic is in the first house and the Moon is in its own sign, the child is born completely without teeth. **12** If malefics are situated at the end of the sign Cancer <term>Karki</term> and are aspected by Mars or the Moon, the child will be born after a long labor or may suffer shortly after. **13** 

If the Ascendant is in a sign ruled by Saturn, or if the Sun, Mars, and Saturn <note>original: "Dhanta"</note> are in Leo or Aries, and if the child is born without the aspect of benefics, it will be born missing limbs or severely disabled <note>original: "maha-ahihine"</note>. **14** When malefic planets are placed at the junctions <term>Sandhi</term> <note>the transitional degrees between two zodiac signs</note> and are not aspected by benefics, the child will be dull-witted or disabled <term>Jada</term>; of this there is no doubt. **15** 

If Leo is the Ascendant and both the Sun and Moon are there, the child will be born blind at night. If benefics and malefics aspect them, the child will have a pair of defective eyes; if the birth is during the day, the left eye <note>original: "vama"</note> will be lost. **16** If the Moon is in the Ascendant, Mars is in the seventh house, and Saturn is in a low position, or if Mars and Venus are together, the child is born with the cord <term>Nalaka</term>. **17** When the Lord of the Ascendant is in the Ascendant, but a malefic is placed there along with it, and the birth occurs during a time of wrath <term>Prarosha</term>, the child is born in captivity or under bonds <term>Vandi</term>. **18** 

If the Moon is in the Ascendant but is not aspected by Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note>, or if the Sun and Moon are together with a malefic, the child is born of a paramour <term>Jarajata</term> <note>meaning the child is illegitimate or born of an extramarital union</note>. **19** If Saturn, Jupiter, and the Moon are in their signs of debilitation <term>Nicha</term>, they also create a birth from a paramour. Or, if the Moon and Venus are in the same house and the Sun, Mercury, and Venus are in another, the same result is predicted. **20**

<meta>This page continues the technical instructions for natal astrology, specifically focusing on the legitimacy of the birth, the physical location where the delivery occurs (temples, forests, or palaces), and determining the sex of the infant based on zodiacal degrees. It follows the previous page's discussion on physical marks like the umbilical cord.</meta>

If the Moon is in a sign ruled by Jupiter <note>original: "Guru-kshetra," referring to Sagittarius or Pisces</note>, or if Jupiter and Venus are in the twelfth house, or if Mercury is in its own house or a forest-like sign, the birth is not of illicit origin <note>original: "na jara-jata," meaning the child is born of a lawful marriage</note>. **21** 

If the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term> is aspected by malefics in the twelfth or sixth houses, it indicates an illicit birth. However, if a benefic planet is situated in a cardinal house <term>Kendra</term> <note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses</note>, the birth is declared legitimate. **22** 

If a benefic is in the Ascendant and in an odd sign, or if Mercury and Venus are in specific portions, the birth occurs near water. **23** 

If Saturn is in the twelfth house and the Moon and Sun are in the sixth house, the birth occurs in a temple <note>original: "devatagare"</note>, a playground, or a place surrounded by water. **24** 

If the Ascendant and its portion are masculine, and Saturn is powerful, the birth may occur in a king's palace, a cow-pen <note>original: "goshtha"</note>, or a traveler’s lodge. **25** 

Depending on whether benefics or Venus are in the cardinal houses, the circumstances of the birth are categorized by the learned sages. **26** 

If the planetary portions are in movable signs <term>Chara</term>, the birth occurs while traveling; if in fixed signs <term>Sthira</term>, the birth occurs in one's own home <note>original: "sviya gehe"</note>. If the signs are dual-natured, the results are mixed. **27** 

When the Ascendant and the Moon's portion are both weak and located in a sign with no other planets, the sages declare that the birth occurs in a desolate or lonely place. **28** 

If the Moon <note>original: "Vidhu"</note> is in the sign of the Sun <note>Leo</note>, or if they are in low degrees, or if Saturn is joined with the Moon in the darkness, the birth takes place in a ditch, a hole, or a subterranean place. **29** 

If aspected by auspicious planets <note>original: "shubhagrahai"</note>, the birth is considered blessed and well-attended. **30** 

If the Ascendant, the Moon, Jupiter, and the Sun are all in odd signs <term>Ojabha</term> <note>Aries, Gemini, Leo, Libra, Sagittarius, Aquarius</note> and in odd-numbered degrees, the child will be male. If they are in even signs and even degrees at the time of conception or birth, the child will be female. **31** 

If the Ascendant is in an odd sign but the Moon is in an even sign, or if the sign Capricorn <term>Makara</term> is powerful, the birth may be atypical; some ancient texts even suggest the appearance of the infant may resemble a monkey <note>original: "vanara"</note> or other creatures if the degrees are highly afflicted. **32**

...if the Sun is in the ninth house <note>the "Dharma" house, also representing the father</note> and situated in a stable sign, it indicates the father is in a fixed location. If it is in a movable sign, he is in a distant land. **11** If the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term> is in a mountainous sign and aspected by planets in the tenth, the father’s status is determined by the strength of those planets. **37** When Jupiter and Venus are in the dual signs <note>Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, or Pisces</note> or if Saturn is in the Ascendant, it indicates the father is away. If the Ascendant is a fixed sign and contains the lord of the ninth, the father is at home. **15** 

If Mars and Saturn are in specific houses, or if Jupiter and Saturn are together, one should speak of the father's passing. If the Sun is in the path of Mars or Saturn, it indicates the father’s death <note>original: "janakasya mrityuh"</note>. **18** 

If the lords of the twelfth and the first houses are weak and defeated in planetary war <term>Graha Yuddha</term>, and if the Sun is in the eighth house, the father will suffer from diseases <note>original: "piturugarditah"</note> at the time of birth. **30** If Saturn is powerful and situated in a certain sign, or if it is aspected by Mars, the father suffers from chronic ailments or wind-based disorders. **18** 

If the planets are in trine <term>Trikona</term> positions, the results vary between day and night. For a birth at night, Saturn takes the role of the father; for a day birth, the Sun <note>original: "Surya"</note> is considered the primary indicator of the father. **35** If malefic planets <term>Papa-kheta</term> are in the ninth house, it signifies a time of fear and death. If the Sun, Saturn, and Mars are in specific houses and not aspected by benefics, it brings great misfortune <term>Arishta</term> to the father. **40** 

### On the Misfortune of the Parents

If the ninth house is occupied by Saturn and other malefic travelers <note>planets</note> are in the eighth house, the father will face sorrow and the child will be born into a troubled state. **41** If Mars is joined with the son of the Sun <note>Saturn; original: "Suryasuta"</note> in a night birth, the father will have passed away before the birth or will depart shortly thereafter. **42** If the Sun is positioned in the eighth house and joined with Saturn and Mars, there is no doubt that the father meets his end at the time of birth. **44**

If malefic planets are in the twelfth, first, and seventh houses from the Ascendant, the mother <note>original: "Janani"</note> dies along with the child; of this, there is no doubt. **44** If malefics are in the sixth and twelfth houses, the mother does not survive. However, if they are in the eighth, seventh, or the Ascendant, and yet a benefic planet aspects them, she may be spared. **45** If Saturn and Mars are in the seventh or in trine, and if Venus is in its exaltation, the mother will have a long life. If Jupiter is in its own house and aspects the seventh or the Ascendant, the mother remains safe from death. <margin>10</margin>

# Tenth Page 10

...regarding the oil in the lamp and the wick: it is known from the Moon. If the Moon is in the hand of a cruel planet at the time of birth, or if it has passed into the hands of others, the child is said to be born in the hands of attendants. **47** 

### Knowledge of the Birthing Room

Now, for the knowledge of the woman in childbirth <term>Sutika</term>: the bed of the mother should be described based on the planets situated in the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term>. If planets are in the middle of a sign, the mother is in the middle of the bed; if in the first half, she is at the head. **48** If the planets are malefic, the bed is old or broken; if they are auspicious <note>original: "shubha"</note>, it is grand and fit for royalty. If mixed, the condition is likewise mixed. 

Regarding the number of attendants: if a planet is in its own zodiac sign <term>Rashi</term>, the number of midwives is tripled. If it is in its divisional ninth-part <term>Navamsha</term>, the number is doubled. **49** Expert astrologers <term>Horavida</term> explain the birth environment through these calculations of the strength of the planets. 

The structure of the room is determined by the zodiac signs: signs like Capricorn <note>original: "Mruga"</note> or the first half of the signs indicate the orientation. Gemini and Sagittarius represent the shape of the room or the bed frame. **50** Some say that the branches or pillars of the house follow the direction of the signs. **51** 

#### Determination of Attendants and Clothing

The number of midwives present is determined thus: Aries and Leo indicate a certain number; Taurus and Scorpio another. For Capricorn, Gemini, Libra, and Virgo, the number of attendants is three. **52** 

One should speak of the mother's clothing <note>original: "matra vastram"</note> based on the lord of the Navamsha of the Ascendant. Similarly, the food consumed by the mother before the birth <note>original: "matra bhojanm"</note> is revealed by the planets. **53** It is said that if planets are in certain houses, the food was honey-like, or perhaps liquid and varied. **54** If the Sun or Mars are dominant, the food was hot or spicy; if the Moon or Venus, it was sweet; if Saturn, it was salty or bitter <note>original: "lavana"</note>. This is understood by the wise through the planetary influences. **55** 

If the Ascendant is in Aries, Leo, or Sagittarius, the mother may have forgotten her surroundings or acted with great passion. If the signs are feminine, she is calm. **56** 

The physical orientation of the newborn's body is also determined here: the head, the feet, and which side the child lies upon. The left foot or the right is determined by the strength of the planets in the third and eleventh houses. **57** 

The location of the door <note>original: "dwaraka"</note> of the birthing room is known from the strongest planet. If no planet is strong, it is determined by the sign itself. **58**

...If there is a defect in the house of the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term>, it is described as being on the left side. According to the teachings of the masters <note>original: "munidaih"</note>, this is divided into three parts. **55** From the wisdom of the sages, the head of the child is known through the position of the planet Shipi-vishta <note>A title for Vishnu, here likely referring to a specific planetary solar influence</note>. Through the calculation of the Ascendant and the lunar days <term>Tithi</term>, the internal state is understood. **50** 

### The Symbolic Lamp of Birth

The lamp in the birthing room follows the direction of the Sun. The amount of oil <note>original: "sneha," which also means affection or fat</note> in that lamp is to be determined from the strength of the Ascendant; this has been stated by the ancients. Peace and the ripening of karma are achieved through these proportions; therefore, for the pleasure of the gods, one should observe the lamp carefully. **51** In the hour <term>Hora</term> of the birth, wherever the lamp is placed, it illuminates the fate. If the planet is in a fixed sign, the lamp remains steady; if it is in a movable sign, the light flickers or is moved. **52** 

When the oil is full, the lamp burns brightly. The wick is understood through the Moon. One should determine the length of the wick from the degree of the Moon in the zodiac sign. Wise men should apply their intellect to understand the state of the wick and the flame. **53** 

### The Position of the Infant

In this way, the "head-yoga" is calculated through the cycle of the Lion <note>referring to the Leo-based calculation or the *Simhasya Chakra*</note>. If the rising sign is a Head-Rising sign <term>Shirshodaya</term>, the birth is head-first. **54** If the sign rises by the feet <term>Prishthodaya</term>, or if malefic planets occupy the signs, there is no doubt that the birth will be reversed or difficult. By the influence of these cruel planets, the mother’s suffering is to be known. **55** 

#### Remedial Actions and Influences

If malefic planets are dominant during the chosen moment <term>Muhurta</term>, one should carefully consider the positions of Saturn and Mars. Through the wisdom of the peaceful mind, one should perform the necessary pacification rituals <note>original: "shanti"</note>. **59** 

When Saturn <note>original: "Ravija," son of the Sun</note> or Mars <note>original: "Kuja," son of the Earth</note> are in the eighth or second house, the purification rituals <term>Samskara</term> for the newborn must be performed quickly. If the Moon is in a diverse or shifting position, the house of the birth is said to be unstable. **60** 

If the Sun is aspected by Mars, or if there is an influence of the wind <note>likely referring to the Vata humor or a specific planetary alignment</note>, the child will be gifted in the science of light <term>Jyotisha</term> as they grow. **61** If the planet is in its highest exaltation, or if it is a day of great spiritual significance, the child will be a protector of the land and build many temples. When the planets are strong and situated in the "bow" <note>Sagittarius or a specific arched formation</note>, the child born among men will be exceptionally wise and possess a great hall of mirrors or deep internal insights. **65** 

All these results regarding the cloth worn, the fruits of the birth, and the prosperity of the lineage are to be declared by the strength of the Sun and other planets when the Sun is in the seventh or tenth house.

When the planets are situated thus, the child takes after them. If the Moon <note>original: "shitarucho," literally 'the cool-rayed one'</note> is strong, the child resembles the mother; if the Sun <note>original: "vivarstvan"</note> is powerful, the child resembles the father. Jupiter <term>Guru</term> also bestows its own likeness and qualities upon the infant. **1**

### Determining Lineage and Appearance

The complexion and character are understood through the position of the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term>. By observing the Sun and the sub-divisions of the signs <term>Navamsha</term>, a wise astrologer should determine the child's caste, the status of their family <term>Kula</term>, and the environment of their birth. **2**

The physical form is mapped across the zodiac in three divisions. In the first division, the sages say the head is represented. In the second division, the neck, the two shoulders, the sides, the chest, and the navel are described. **3** In the third division, the lower belly, the secret parts, the thighs, the knees, the shanks, and the feet are located. These parts are assigned to the left or right side of the body based on the movement of the zodiacal wheel <term>Chakra</term>. **4**

### Physical Marks and Scars

A scar or wound <term>Vrana</term> occurs if a malefic planet is present in a specific house. If a beneficial planet is present or casts its gaze <term>Drishti</term> upon it, there will be a mole or a lucky mark <term>Lakshma</term> instead. If the planet is in a fixed sign, the mark is permanent; if in a movable sign, it may change or fade. **5** 

If the Sun is the influence, the mark is dark or variegated. If it is Saturn <note>original: "Suryaputra," the son of the Sun</note>, the mark is on the feet or legs. If Mercury <note>original: "Chandraja," the son of the Moon</note> is present, the face is beautiful and the mark is auspicious. If Mars <note>original: "Bhumiputra," the son of the Earth</note> influences the house, there is a mark from a wound. If Rahu <note>original: "Tamoga," the traveler in darkness</note> is present, the mark is faint or hidden. **8**

The specific location of these marks is determined by the Moon’s position relative to the "Person of Time" <note>original: "Kalapurusha," the concept of the zodiac as a human body</note>. These marks, whether moles or small spots, signify the peace or turmoil of the individual’s destiny. **11**

Where a gentle planet casts its sight, the mark is pleasing. Where a harsh planet rules or gazes, a scar or a deformity may be found. This knowledge is confirmed in the Great Essence of Astrology <note>original: "Hora Sara"</note> and the Light of Shambhu’s Astrology <note>original: "Shambhu Hora Prakasha"</note>. Thus ends the Chapter on Birth. **7**

# The Light of Shambhu’s Astrology: The Chapter on Birth
# ## The Results of the Houses

### The Fruits of the Twelve Houses of Birth

Now we shall describe the results for a person born under the twelve houses. Through the soft and rhythmic words of the ancient masters, these truths are revealed to those who seek them. **1** The physical form, the complexion, the courage, and the caste of the individual are all revealed through the strength of these positions...

For men, the body, the father, or the mother are influenced by the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term> and one’s own intelligence. **2** Every planet, if it occupies two houses or is a benefic <term>Saumya</term> <note>original: "Saumya" refers to "gentle" planets like Jupiter, Venus, or a waxing Moon</note>, is capable of bestowing results. If a planet is in its own house and well-placed among all these, the person becomes a great ruler of a kingdom. **3**

### Character and Longevity

If the Ascendant is strong and occupied by auspicious signs, the person becomes a protector of their family and a destroyer of enemy clans. Such a person is endowed with grace and playfulness, lives a life of morality, and possesses a long life span due to their inherent strength. **4**

When benefics <term>Saumya</term> are situated in the houses of enemies <note>the 6th house</note> from the Ascendant, while malefics <term>Krura</term> <note>original: "Krura," meaning "cruel" or "harsh" planets like Mars or Saturn</note> are well-placed, the person lives a long life, becomes the husband of many women, and is fearless and detached from worldly passions <note>original: "vitaraga," a term often used for monks or those who have transcended desire</note>. **5**

### Beauty and Affliction

If all the benefics are situated in the Ascendant, the person born is handsome, always humble, and well-behaved. However, if all the malefics are present there, the person is plagued by diseases <term>Vyadhi</term>, grief, and constant fear. **6**

When the Ascendant is strong, the person is physically robust. If a malefic is situated there in its own division, or if the Moon <note>original: "shitaruchi," the cool-rayed one</note> is combined with malefics, the person may be prone to injuring others or being weak in certain limbs. **7**

### Physical Ailments and Signs

In the various divisions of the Ascendant, if a malefic is present, there will be pain or a disorder in the left side of the head or the right side of the waist. This is a certainty. **8** If Rahu <note>the North Node of the Moon</note>, Mars <term>Bhauma</term>, or the Sun <term>Arka</term> are in the head region, there is a risk of injury from weapons or fire. If a malefic is in the house of death and the Ascendant is also aspected by a malefic, there is a double fear of suffering from burns or fire. **9**

The physical form <term>Akriti</term>, the innate nature <term>Prakriti</term>, flaws, virtues, tastes, and even the type of village or occupation one resides in are determined by these planetary positions. **10** According to the ancient sages, the victory or success of a person should be understood through the Lord of the Ascendant or the planets dwelling within it. **11**

### The Stages of Planetary Power

A malefic planet is considered "young," while a benefic is a "child." Others are considered middle-aged. Saturn <note>original: "Sani"</note> is often seen as slow or aged. **12** The Sun <note>original: "Prabhakara"</note> and Mars govern the father's side and strength. Jupiter <term>Guru</term> and Mercury <note>original: "Jna"</note> relate to wisdom and the battlefield. This is the opinion of the kings and the Greeks <term>Yavana</term> <note>original: "Yavana"; this refers to Greek astrological influence which historically integrated with Indian Jyotisha</note>. **13** Whatever planet is strongest in the Ascendant determines the person's character. **14**

<margin>12</margin>

If a benefic is in the Ascendant, the person is said to be youthful in spirit. If the Moon and Venus are in the Ascendant, the person... <unclear>is destroyed</unclear>.

### Character and Social Standing

If a youth is rich in knowledge **15**, and even if they are associated with an elderly person’s Ascendant <term>Lagna</term> <note>original: "Lagna," the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon</note>, they will possess a nature that is respected and honored by all people. **18** <note>The manuscript numbering jumps here from 15 to 18</note>

If the planets Saturn <note>original: "Shani"</note> and the Shadow Planet Rahu <note>the north node of the moon</note> are positioned in certain houses, there is a risk of the destruction of the family's wealth. **17**

### On Speech and Disposition

If the planets are positioned in the Ascendant or the second house, the person’s speech <note>original: "Vani"</note> will be like that of a courtesan—highly polished, charming, and pleasing to others. Their words will be sweet and bolstered by truth. **18**

If the Moon is situated in these same positions, that person will be a lover of jewels and will prefer the company of excellent men. This is the result for the second house; let there be no doubt in this analysis. **19**

### Planetary Influences on Personality

If Venus <note>original: "Bhargava"</note> is located in the Ascendant or the lord of the Ascendant is in a specific house, the person will be of a modest nature and always fond of small or subtle things. **20**

If Rahu, Saturn, and Ketu <note>the south node of the moon</note> are all located in the Ascendant, the person’s nature should be known as unstable or lowly, yet they will be a lover of flavors and sensory pleasures. **21**

When the "Lord of the Body" <note>the planet ruling the first house</note> is associated with a harsh or malefic planet, the person becomes prone to intense anger. Such a person is truly born to be wrathful and fond of the sound of battle drums. **22**

### Health and Vitality

Through the influence of gentle planets <note>original: "Saumya," such as Jupiter or Mercury</note> in the Ascendant, the person is well-proportioned. However, if they are under the influence of "sinful" or malefic planets, or the Sun, they will be plagued by constant doubts and fears. **33** <note>The OCR notes this numeral 33 likely refers to verse 23</note>

If the lord of the Ascendant is weak or influenced by certain moving planets, the person may suffer from rapid-onset diseases, or troubles related to women. Fear and physical wasting may occur. **24**

If the Sun and Mars <note>original: "Ravi" and "Bhoma"</note> are in the Ascendant, or if the lord of the Ascendant is strong but influenced by them, the person will have a temperament like fire and will be afflicted by bile <term>Pitta</term> <note>original: "Pitta," one of the three "doshas" or humors in traditional medicine, associated with heat and metabolism</note>. **25**

### The Study of the Second House

This concludes the analysis of the **House of the Body** <term>Tanu Bhava</term>. Now, I shall describe the **Second House** <term>Dvitiyam</term>, which concerns **Wealth** <term>Dhana</term>.

This house governs the voice and tone of the person, the quality of their speech, and their success in buying and selling. It also dictates their fortune regarding pearls, silver, and other riches. **1**

The wise should carefully observe the position of the lord of the Ascendant and Venus. If they are associated with auspicious signs, the state of the person's treasury will be successful and full. **2**

When planets move through the House of Wealth, one must judge the results—whether they bring an ocean of auspiciousness or the influence of "cruel" planets. One must analyze if the results will be inverted based on the strength of the planetary lords. **3**

If the Sun and Leo are in contact with the House of Wealth, the person will possess a treasury like a king's. They will be like a hero among men, or like one who has conquered their senses, marked by distinct and noble characteristics. **4** ...as the voice...

### Wealth and Planetary Afflictions

When the Shadow Planet Rahu <term>Rahu</term> <note>The North Node of the Moon, considered a "severing" or chaotic influence in Vedic astrology</note> is situated in the House of Wealth <term>Dhanabhava</term> <note>original: "Dhanabhava," the 2nd house of a natal chart</note>, it makes the person irritable and prone to anger. Under this influence, the <unclear>cool-rayed one</unclear> <note>the Moon</note> ... <unclear>text corrupted</unclear> ... this terrible placement may destroy the family's prospects. **5**

Regarding those born with the son of Bhrigu <note>original: "Bhrigu's son," a common epithet for the planet Venus</note> in the House of Wealth, if it is joined with the essence of Rahu, it leads to <unclear>unclear</unclear> and affects the wealth of men. **6**

### Physical Health and Diet

In the birth chart <term>Jataka</term>, if there is a specific affliction, the person may suffer from eye diseases or secret defects. However, despite these difficulties, they will be fond of sweet foods <note>original: "mishtanna"</note> and possess wealth, though they may be dear to those who are troublesome. **7**

### Influence on Relatives

The father, grandfather, born wife, and maternal uncle are influenced by the Sun <note>original: "Ravi"</note> and other planetary lords. If these lords are joined together, the person’s wife may be <unclear>unclear</unclear>. **8**

The Lord of the Son <note>the 5th house ruler</note>, the wife, Jupiter <note>original: "Guru"</note>, daughters, sisters, the mother’s sister, maternal uncles, and paternal uncles are all to be judged as being like the father in this context. **9**

If the Lord of the Father's House is situated in the sixth house <note>the house of debts, enemies, and illness</note>, or in the house of a <unclear>hostile planet</unclear>, then wealth or money will remain in the hands of the brother-in-law, and the person will be free from sorrow regarding it. **10**

### Ailments of the Eyes and Face

If malefic planets <term>Krura</term> <note>original: "Krura," literally "cruel" planets like Mars or Saturn</note> are positioned in the House of Wealth, it results in a wound or affliction to the eyes <note>original: "netre"</note>. If the Sun and Saturn <note>original: "Savita" and "Atmaja"</note> are placed there, it leads to a very painful condition. **11**

When there is excessive wealth but also the pain of weeping or disease, and the <unclear>node</unclear> is in the House of Wealth, the person may experience anger and a lack of coolness in their eyes. <note>original: "sa-tvisha tamra," perhaps referring to a copper-like or bloodshot appearance</note> **12**

If Venus <note>original: "Sita"</note> is joined with malefic planets in that position, it causes a defect in the left eye. If the Sun is in the second house, the third <unclear>part</unclear> is destroyed by the cruel planets. **13**

If Venus is in the houses of wealth or expenditure <note>2nd or 12th houses</note>, it makes a man prone to intense pain. If the Moon <note>original: "Shitamaya"</note> and Jupiter <note>original: "Guru"</note> are also positioned there, the ancient sages <note>original: "muni"</note> say the result is as follows: **14**

If a planet is in the Treasury House <note>the 2nd house</note>, the person will be a great orator or speaker <term>Vaghmi</term>, possessing a gentle yet curved speech. If Ketu <note>the South Node of the Moon</note> is joined there with the Lord of the sign, they are said to be <unclear>unclear</unclear>. **15**

If the seven <unclear>planets</unclear> are in the House of Wealth, or if it is joined with the enemy of a planet, it causes diseases of the mouth <note>original: "mukha roga"</note>, as heard from the ancient traditions. **16**

When planets are in the House of Wealth, the person’s mother or wife may suffer from a persistent disease or the wealth itself may be diminished through such illnesses. **17**

### Conclusion of the House of Wealth

According to the Yavanas <note>original: "Yavana," referring to the Greco-Indian astrological tradition which heavily influenced early Indian horoscopy</note>, there is a loss of wealth. **17** 

If the Lord of the Wealth House is situated in an adverse position, or if Mars <note>original: "Bhauma," the son of the Earth</note> is present there, it causes the destruction of wealth. This loss may result from royal punishment, thieves, or the sharp gaze of enemies. **18** 

A specific affliction to the skin's complexion or trouble through women may arise from the wrath of fire or Mars <note>original: "Araka," a common name for the planet Mars</note>. These effects are well-known in the world when Mars is situated in the House of Wealth. **19** 

**Thus ends the consideration of the House of Wealth.**
