### The House of Siblings and Prowess

Now, the results of the House of Siblings <term>Sahajabhava</term> <note>The 3rd house in a natal chart, governing brothers, sisters, courage, and physical strength</note> as spoken by the authority Shashishekhara <note>literally "the one with the moon in his hair," an epithet for Shiva, suggesting the divine origin of these teachings</note> will be explained. If a malefic planet is situated in this third house, the following results are declared. **1**

One should determine the welfare of those born together <note>siblings</note>, one's own prowess and courage, as well as the condition of the mother, paternal uncles, and maternal uncles through the study of the House of Siblings and its lords. **2**

If this house is joined with or aspected by benefic planets <note>original: "Saumya," referring to gentle planets like Jupiter, Venus, or a strong Moon</note> or is placed in a benefic sign, all these matters lead to auspiciousness. However, if it is occupied by malefic planets <note>original: "Papa," such as Saturn, Mars, or Rahu</note>, there is surely a lack of brothers for the individual. **3**

If Saturn <note>original: "Manda," the slow-moving one</note> aspects this house, the younger siblings who are born may perish. If Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva," the giver of life</note> or Mars aspects it, there will surely be great difficulty or fear concerning the younger siblings. **4**

If the Lord of the Third House <term>Vikramapa</term> <note>literally "the Lord of Prowess"</note> is associated with the elder siblings, they will surely prosper. At such a time, the man becomes particularly endowed with enjoyments and worldly possessions. **5**

If Saturn <note>original: "Ravija," the son of the Sun</note> is joined with another planet in this house, the strength of the younger sibling is diminished. It also creates anxiety in the mind or causes diseases arising from an imbalance of the wind <note>Vata disorders, which in traditional Indian medicine relate to the nervous system and joints</note>. **6**

If the Sun <note>original: "Ravi"</note> is present, or if the sibling's planet is "combust" <note>rendered invisible and weak by being too close to the Sun's rays</note>, it causes fear of broken bones, poison, or injuries from fire. If a benefic planet is strong there, these evils are averted. **7**

If the Lord of the Third is in its own house or is aspected by a friendly planet, one has good brothers and sisters. If it is aspected by the <gloss>enemy</gloss> <note>original: "ri," likely an abbreviation for "ripu" or enemy</note> or situated in a hostile house, the outcome must be judged accordingly. **8**

If the Lord of that house is Saturn, it brings joy through the younger brother. However, if it is aspected by Mars and the lord of the house is debilitated <note>original: "Neecha," the zodiacal point where a planet's influence is at its lowest</note>, the siblings will suffer from various diseases. **9**

If the Moon is in the house of siblings, very few siblings will perish; one sibling might be long-lived or perhaps become equal to a king. **10**

If the Moon is aspected by malefics in the third house, the destruction of brothers is indicated, which is not auspicious. If, however, it is endowed with strength...

<margin>Shai. 14</margin> ...the disease of younger siblings, physical strength, and others born together—this is what the wise call the House of Siblings <note>original: "sahajanna," referring to those born from the same womb</note>. **11** 

If Mars <note>original: "Lohita," the Red One</note> is placed in the third house, and is joined by or aspected by cruel planets, the person will suffer from diseases. Through the science of astrology, one should predict diseases arising from excess bile <term>pitta</term> <note>One of the three "doshas" or humors in Ayurvedic medicine, associated with fire and digestion</note> or skin eruptions for the younger sibling. **12** 

If cruel planets joined with enemies are in the third house, there is a lack of happiness. The person may suffer from paralysis <note>original: "pakshaghata," a stroke or palsy</note>, wind-born diseases <term>vayu</term>, or general pain. Their wife may also suffer an injury or affliction to the hand. **13** 

If Saturn <note>original: "Arkasuta," the son of the Sun</note> is located in the middle decan <term>drekkana</term> <note>A 10-degree division of a zodiac sign</note>, one should declare that two brothers or two pregnancies were lost. If the lord of the third house is joined with a planet in a watery sign, there is a danger of drowning in a river. **14** 

If Mars <note>original: "Mahija," son of the Earth</note> is in the eleventh house and Saturn <note>original: "Arkasuta"</note> is in the seventh house, and the person is currently undergoing a specific planetary period, the wise should predict the loss of three siblings. **15** 

If Jupiter <note>original: "Guru"</note>, Venus <note>original: "Bhriguja"</note>, or Mercury <note>original: "Budha"</note> is in the third house, or in the house of happiness, there is joy for the siblings. However, if they are joined by the lords of the houses of thieves or strength, the wise should then predict the destruction of two siblings. **16** 

If the Sun and Rahu <note>the north node of the moon</note> are in the third house, or if it is occupied by benefic planets but the third house itself is afflicted, there will be the loss of three sisters. Furthermore, there may be trouble in the arms or the belly. **17** 

If there is only one sister, and Venus <note>original: "Sita"</note> is placed in the eighth house or between malefic planets, one should predict a fear of injury to the hand or a similar affliction. **18** 

The condition of the mother and those born from her should be judged by the planets in these positions. Such is the lineage and the characteristics of the younger siblings that should be spoken of by wise astrologers <note>original: "horikai," practitioners of Horoscopy</note> using their intellect. **19**

### Thus ends the consideration of the Third House.

The following are the results of a planet residing in the third house for a person. If joined with a friendly planet or aspected by a friend, the person will possess various villages and lands. **1** 

The condition of large tanks, wells, and trees <note>referring to the person's estates and public works</note> should be judged by the great ones through the third house. If the lord of this house is joined with or aspected by benefics, there will be great happiness at that time. **2** 

If the lord of the third house is strong and situated in the third house itself, it grants auspicious results. However, if the lord is set <note>combust, or too close to the sun</note>, in its sign of debilitation <term>neecha</term>, or defeated in planetary war, the opposite and negative results should be considered. **3** 

If Jupiter is in the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term>, Saturn in the third, and Rahu is in a very fortunate position, then the family and attendants will thrive if the planets in the Ascendant and the twelfth house are benefic. **4** 

If the Sun is in the third house and is strong, it wards off old age; <margin>14</margin> however, if afflicted, the person may suffer from wounds. They may experience pain from ulcers or wounds on the feet, the hands, or the mouth. **5** 

Regarding the paternal grandfather... or if there is enmity... and if Jupiter is present...

...If the son of the Sun <note>original: "Suryaputra," referring to Saturn</note> is placed in the **Fourth House**, the mother <note>original: "janitri"</note> may suffer from ailments caused by an imbalance of the wind humor <term>vayu-dosha</term> <note>One of the three Ayurvedic humors; an excess of vayu/wind leads to joint pain, bloating, or nervous disorders</note>. If the Lord of the Night <note>original: "Yamini-ishena," the Moon</note> is also situated there, it indicates various diseases. **7** 

One should predict the death of a sibling <note>original: "sahottha"</note> through a fall from a tree, or by drowning in water, or by their own hand. If the Moon is joined with Saturn <note>original: "Manda"</note> or the Sun <note>original: "Ravi"</note>, the mother will suffer from chronic illnesses. **8** 

If cruel planets are located in the **Fourth House**, whether in a village or a city, the house may be consumed by fire <note>original: "vahni"</note>. **9** 

When Mars <note>original: "Bhauma"</note> occupies the House of the Mother <note>the Fourth House</note> and is aspected by Saturn, she will suffer from illness; if there is a further affliction, the mother may be of wayward conduct <note>original: "abhicharini," historically referring to a woman who deviates from traditional moral or social codes</note>. **10** 

If Venus <note>original: "Shukra"</note> or Jupiter <note>original: "Ijya"</note> are in the Fourth House, there will be an abundance of water <note>original: "nire-patu"</note>. If Mercury <note>original: "Saumya"</note> is mixed there, it indicates the presence of wells and step-wells <note>original: "kupa-vapya"</note>. If Saturn is present, the water will be bitter <note>original: "tiktam"</note>; if the Sun or Mars are present, the water in the land will be brackish or salty. **11** 

If the Lord of the Fourth is with the Sun, or if the Fourth House is occupied by Mercury and aspected by benefics, the person lives in a grand residence. If the Moon and Venus are present, it is a palace. If Saturn is there, it is an old house; if Mars, it is a house built of timber or scorched by fire. **12** 

If Saturn is in a sign where it possesses strength <term>virya</term>, the person will have a strong physical frame. If the Sun is in the Fourth House, the person may be prone to diseases and may fall from their position <note>original: "sthana-bhrishta"</note>; if Mars is there, the person will be similarly sickly. **13** 

When the Lord of the Fourth is in the Fourth House itself along with Jupiter and Venus, the house will have many wells. At that time, the person enjoys comfort, though there may be strong winds <note>original: "vayu"</note> or digestive troubles. **14** 

If Mercury and the Sun are together, the home may feature copper <note>original: "tamra"</note>; if Jupiter is there, tin <note>original: "trapu"</note>; if the son of the Sun <note>Saturn</note> is there, it indicates iron <note>original: "loha"</note> or heavy materials. **15** 

If Jupiter is in the tenth and Venus in the second, and the son of the Moon <note>Mercury</note> is in a watery sign, the house is built of bricks <note>original: "ishtika"</note>. If the sign is a four-legged sign <note>such as Aries or Leo</note>, the house will be near a place for livestock. If Saturn is near the residence, the house may be made of wood or straw. **16** 

Planets located in the Fourth House that possess full strength <term>virya</term> give their full results as described. However, if these planets are located in their debilitated divisions <term>nichamsha</term> <note>the specific degrees where a planet is at its weakest</note>, the person will not experience these benefits. **17** 

If benefic planets <term>shubha-graha</term> are in the Fourth House with strength, the person enjoys happiness and the company of their mother. Such a person is self-controlled, wise, and devoted to the learned <note>original: "vipra-parayanah," specifically those dedicated to sacred knowledge</note>. **18** 

If malefic planets <note>original: "papa"</note> are in the Fourth House and it is not aspected by the Moon <note>original: "shitadyuti," the cold-rayed one</note>, there is no happiness. The person may lose their mother early. If benefics are present, the opposite should be predicted. **19** 

...If the Fourth House is aspected by the three... <note>text cuts off</note>

...If the [Sun] is not aspected by benefic planets, it results in the death of the father <term>janaka</term> <note>original: "janaka-anāśah"</note>. Similarly, it is said that if the Lord of the Night <note>original: "Yāminīśa," referring to the Moon</note> is so placed, it brings about the death of the mother for men. **1** If the Sun is in the Tenth House <term>meshurana</term> <note>From the Greek 'mesouranema' for mid-heaven; this term reflects the historical exchange between Greek and Indian astrology</note> and is aspected by Saturn <note>original: "Arka-drishtah" usually refers to the Sun, but "Manda" or "Sani" is implied here by the context of "cruel" aspect</note>, it causes the destruction of the person born <term>jātaka</term>, leading to great poverty and loss of status through illness. **11** 

If a planet is in its sign of debilitation <term>nīcha</term> but is endowed with strength, or if a cruel planet is situated in the heart <note>The Fourth House traditionally represents the chest/heart in the physical body</note>, the person may experience cruelty. If Saturn <note>original: "Manda"</note> is positioned there, the person’s character becomes known to others slowly. **12** 

If only one cruel planet is present, the mother may be of a difficult nature; however, if a benefic planet <term>saumya</term> occupies the House of the Mother <note>the Fourth House</note>, the person will have but one mother <note>suggesting the mother's longevity or the absence of stepmothers</note>. **13**

**Thus ends the consideration of one's nature.** 

Now, I shall truly explain the **fruits of the Fifth House** for men, which brings joy. This house indicates the state of progeny <term>santāna</term> and the womb, the number of children, the attainment of mantras, humility, and knowledge <term>vidyā</term>. **1** 

One’s intellect <term>buddhi</term> should be understood entirely from this house. If the Lord of this house is strong and the planet Jupiter <note>original: "Gīrvāṇa," a name for the teacher of the gods</note> is well-placed, all things will be auspicious and beautiful for the person born. **2** 

If Mars <note>original: "Bhauma"</note> is situated in the House of Knowledge <note>the 5th house</note>, the person is filled with anger. While they may be a giver of knowledge, they are also prone to wrath. Like a jackal among men, they may possess a poisonous or harsh speech. Such a person is said to be devoted to their wife, yet they may suffer from the loss of children as previously described, or face constant distress. **3** 

If Saturn <note>original: "Manda"</note> is situated here, the person may suffer from diseases caused by worms <note>original: "krimi"</note> or ailments of the wind humor <term>vāta-gulma</term> <note>An Ayurvedic term for abdominal tumors or bloating caused by excess 'wind'</note>. If the Sun <note>original: "Aditya"</note> or Saturn <note>original: "Arkaja"</note> are present, the intellect is fickle or dull, and the person may be prone to anger. **4** 

When benefic planets occupy the House of Intellect <term>Dhi-sthana</term>, the person is a creator of great things, humble, and consistently of good character in their service to others. However, if cruel planets are there, the person is blocked by the world, prone to anger, lacks knowledge of the scriptures, and is obsessed with worldly schemes while criticizing the virtuous. **5** 

The Sun in this house, due to its heat, burns the womb of the mother, leading to the loss of pregnancy or the death of offspring, leaving the person without children. If Saturn <note>original: "Manda"</note> is there, there is a risk of diseases from worms or blood-related disorders <term>raktārti</term> caused by sharp imbalances. **6** 

If Mercury <note>original: "Budha"</note> is in the House of Sons, there will be female offspring <note>original: "kanyāpatyah"</note>. If Venus <note>original: "Bhārgava"</note> is there, the person is joined by many daughters and sons. If Jupiter <note>original: "Deva-pujya"</note>, the teacher of the gods, is there, the person will be endowed with truly virtuous sons...

<meta>This page continues the transition from the Fourth House (parents) to the Fifth House, which primarily governs progeny, children, and creative intellect. The text lists various planetary combinations (yogas) that predict the birth of sons, daughters, or the loss of children, reflecting the high importance of lineage in historical Indian society.</meta>

Afterward, if joined with a benefic <term>saumya</term>... **8** If the Son of Earth <note>original: "Bhumiputra," referring to the planet Mars</note> is situated in the House of the Father <note>the Ninth House</note>, it destroys the father. However, if it is joined with the Teacher of the Gods <note>original: "Daivapujya," referring to Jupiter</note>, then the person is born with great radiance <term>tejas</term> and is not like those who came before him. **9**

If the Lord of the Seventh House is in the House of Children <term>sutasthana</term> <note>the Fifth House</note>, or even if it is a friend, when it is aspected by cruel <term>krura</term> <note>malefic planets like Mars or Saturn</note> planets, then a miscarriage <term>garbhachyuti</term> will occur. **10**

If a malefic planet is in its own sign and aspected by other malefics while in the House of Children, one is devoid of offspring. However, if that house is joined with strength or if a benefic planet occupies the sign of the child, a man will surely have sons. **11**

If Saturn <term>Manda</term> is in the house of intellect <term>dhistha</term> <note>another name for the Fifth House</note>, there is no offspring due to miscarriage. If the Sun <note>original: "Surya"</note> is there, the children may be stillborn <term>mritapatya</term>, or the wife may suffer. If Rahu or Ketu are present, the man may have a "bad son" <term>kuputra</term> <note>referring to a child who brings dishonor to the family</note>. **12**

If the Lord of the Ascendant <term>ittham-isha</term> is situated in the Fifth House, it grants the man a wealth of sons. If this position is aspected by benefics, he will find great happiness through his two sons. **13**

If the Sun is in an even sign, or if Mercury <term>Budha</term> is in its division <term>navamsha</term> <note>a one-ninth divisional chart used for fine-tuning predictions</note>, or if it is aspected by the Moon <note>original: "Shitadyuti," literally "the cold-rayed one"</note>, then many daughters <term>kanya-praja</term> will surely be born to the man. **14**

If the divisions of Venus <term>Sita</term> or Mercury occupy the House of Children and are aspected by them, many daughters will be born. But if they are situated in odd signs, sons will surely be born to the man. **15**

If Venus <term>Bhriguja</term> is in its own division but not aspected by Venus, or if the Moon <term>Himamshu</term> is joined with or aspected by Jupiter <term>Ijya</term> and Venus in the House of Children, many offspring are predicted. **16**

If the House of Children is in Saturn's division and joined with or aspected by the Moon and Saturn, the man will surely have a son who is "born again" <note>likely referring to a child from a second marriage</note> or an adopted son <term>dattaka</term>. **17**

If Saturn is joined with the Moon and Mercury, a "purchased son" <term>krita</term> <note>an ancient category of legal sonship where a child is bought from parents</note> is predicted. If the sign belongs to Mars (the Son of Earth) or the Sun... **18**

When Jupiter and others aspect the house, there is great auspiciousness and gain. If joined with benefics, the wife will be full of love and strength. If joined with cruel planets, she will be lacking in affection. **19**

As many malefic planets as there are aspecting the House of Children, so many pregnancies will be lost. If the house is aspected by benefics, the outcome is excellent. **20**

As many planets as there are in the sky aspecting the House of Children, some say that is the number of children...

<margin>Page 16</margin> This lineage shall be known by the wise. If female planets <note>original: "stri-nabhogai," referring to the Moon or Venus</note> are positioned there, daughters will be born. **21** If three sons are born but the planetary portions are influenced by dance or music, and if they are aspected by benefic <term>saumya</term> planets, they are said to be beyond reproach. One should know the children through the bodily planets. **22** 

Through a mixture of planetary aspects, the progeny will be of mixed gender <note>both sons and daughters</note>. As many planets as there are influencing the "leader of sons" <note>the planet governing the 5th house</note>, that is how many children there shall be. If the lord of the house is accompanied by benefics, the pregnancy will be successful. **23** 

This was spoken by Lord Shiva himself to the Goddess Durga; having been told to the Sun-god <note>original: "Aruna"</note>, its influence is great. It should be meditated upon by those who know the science of hours <term>hora-vid</term> <note>astrologers</note> and the movements of the planets. **24** 

Regarding the house of sons: some lords of astrology say that if Mars <note>original: "Bhauma"</note> is in the house of sons, even in its own sign, it causes the destruction of sons. If a benefic planet is present, there is a delay in the happiness of having children. **25** 

Fie upon it! If Venus <note>original: "Shukra"</note> is there, it does not lead to... <unclear>wa</unclear>... whether it be a son or the son’s brother. If Venus and Rahu <note>the North Node of the Moon</note> are together in that place, the son will suffer from diseases caused by bile <term>pitta</term>. **26** 

When Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note> is in the house of intellect <note>the 5th house</note> or in its own divisional chart <term>varga</term>, or if the seven planets are in benefic divisions or aspected by benefics, a noble son will be born. The opposite occurs if influenced by cruel <term>krura</term> <note>malefic</note> planets. **27** 

The Sun <note>original: "Ravi"</note> gives one son or a favorable yoga for children. Two daughters are born if the sign is a feminine one. **28** 

Jupiter is the giver of sons in the five-fold division, while Venus <note>original: "Sita"</note> gives daughters. Saturn <note>original: "Shani"</note> gives... <unclear>wa</unclear>... a son with difficulty. If two planets are joined with Ketu <note>the South Node</note> or aspected by Rahu, twins may be born. **29**

Thus ends the consideration of the Seventh House. <note>The text labels this the 7th House, though the content has focused on the 5th House of children. This may reflect an older or variant system of house counting, or a scribal error.</note>

Now, the results of the Sixth House are described, as they relate to the lives of humans. Starting from the top of the head: it governs women who perform cruel deeds, maternal uncles <term>matala</term>, and enemies <term>ripu</term>. **1** 

Anxiety and doubt—all this should be considered regarding the Sixth House by the flourishing astrologers. If the lord of that house is endowed with strength, then everything related to that house will be auspicious. **2** 

If the house of enemies is in a benefic sign, joined with benefics, or aspected by benefics, it is declared to be auspicious. However, if it is joined with or aspected by cruel <term>krura</term> planets, it shall not be auspicious. **3** 

Whether it is the Moon, Venus, Mercury, or Jupiter, if they are strong in the house of enemies, the person will possess wealth and go forth with the gods. If the Sun or Mars <note>original: "Rudhira," meaning the Red Planet</note> are there, the person will possess animals, such as cattle and elephants.

<meta>This page transitions from the analysis of the Fifth House (children) to the Sixth House, traditionally known as the House of Enemies, Debts, and Diseases (Shatru-bhava). It continues the pattern of describing how specific planetary placements affect not just the individual, but their extended family, particularly maternal relatives.</meta>

<margin>Page 17</margin> ...If Saturn <note>original: "Shani"</note> is positioned in the sixth house in a Shudra sign, it brings weakness to the elder brother and the maternal uncle <term>matula</term> <note>Sanskrit: matula; the mother's brother, an important figure in traditional family structures</note>. Through the cause of some secret or known reason, death may occur by falling from a height or by water. **4**

Alternatively, if the Sun <note>original: "Surya"</note> is situated in the house of enemies <note>the 6th house</note>, the person will be well-versed in the sacred scriptures <note>original: "shastra-bhijnata"</note>. However, this placement causes great agitation to the person and their maternal uncle, arising from fierce and uncontrolled anger. **5**

If the Moon or Venus <note>original: "Chandra" or "Shukra"</note> is located in the house of enemies, the maternal uncle will have only female children. The individual themselves, however, will be endowed with vigor and their own offspring will be happy and possess great beauty and splendor. **7**

Regarding the house of valor <note>the 3rd house, which governs siblings</note>: if malefic planets are present there, it is said to cause pain to the brothers. One should know that death or suffering for the maternal uncles and siblings is predicted by the presence of cruel planets in the third house. **8**

When female planets <note>original: "stri-vicara," referring to the Moon or Venus</note> enter the house of enemies, it is said that happiness will come to the sister. If strong benefic <term>saumya</term> <note>saumya: literally "gentle" or "moon-like," referring to planets like Jupiter, Venus, or a well-placed Mercury</note> planets are present, one should predict that the maternal aunt will be blessed with both a husband and sons. **9**

If the house is occupied by a mixture of planets—some cruel and some kind—then the results will be mixed. One should always predict the nature of a person's enemies based on the type of planet that occupies or aspects the house: **10**

| Planet in 6th House | Caste/Nature of the Enemy |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Sun** | A Warrior <note>original: "Kshatriya"</note> |
| **Moon** | A Laborer <note>original: "Shudra"</note> |
| **Mercury** | A Woman |
| **Jupiter** | A Priest <note>original: "Devapujya/Brahmin"</note> |
| **Venus** | A Merchant <note>original: "Vaishya"</note> |
| **Rahu or Saturn** | An Outcaste <note>original: "Yajsh"</note> |

**11**

If the Sun or Saturn is in the house of enemies, there may be a blemish, a mark, or a scar on the skin. In that same area of the body, there may be pain caused by "wind" <note>original: "vayu," referring to Vata-type ailments like joint pain or neurological issues</note>. For women, this placement should be read as a specific physical affliction. **12**

If Mars <note>original: "Sitije," here likely referring to the red planet</note> or the Sun is in the sixth house, even if the man is learned in the scriptures, he will be "dull" or slow in some respects. He will suffer from pain caused by fire, poison, or the breaking of bones <note>original: "asthi-bhanga"</note>. Furthermore, he may suffer from diseases of the blood. There may be a visible mark or injury on the thighs or specifically on the right foot. **13**

If Mars and Venus are together, there is a singular bond with the mother's side of the family. However, if the Sun also aspects this place, the person will suffer from enemies. **14**

If the Sun is joined or aspected by Jupiter <note>original: "Mantrina," the counselor</note> in the sixth house, the man will face fears. There may be a loss of wealth through animals or deformity. This can be caused by the influence of Saturn or other factors. **15**

If the Moon, Mercury, or Jupiter is in the house of enemies, it certainly provides the man with a comfortable bed <note>meaning a life of relative physical comfort</note>. However, if Saturn is also there, one should predict ailments or difficulties for the **maternal uncle**.

...secretly **18**. If the Moon <note>original: "Chandra"</note> is with a companion in the house of wealth <note>the 2nd house</note>, one should speak of a pair of maternal uncles <term>matula</term> <note>maternal uncles are often analyzed through the 6th house and its relationship to the 3rd</note>. If Mars <note>original: "Bhauma"</note> is in the sixth house, it is known that there is but one maternal uncle; however, there is a danger of a sudden blow or injury arising from the fear of enemies. **17**

If Venus <note>original: "Shukra"</note> is in the house of enemies <note>the 6th house</note>, then there will be a maternal aunt <note>literally "mother's sister"</note>. The astrologer <term>daiva-vedin</term> <note>literally "one who knows the divine/fate"</note> should declare this regarding three or even two relatives. **18** **Thus ends the consideration of the House of Enemies.**

Now I shall declare the results <term>phala</term> regarding the nature of the spouse <note>original: "kalatra," referring to the 7th house of marriage</note>. One should examine the partner, the journeys, and the physical form through this house. **1** If benefic planets are present in these signs, or if the signs are joined by benefics, it is auspicious. If joined by malefic planets, it will not be good. If Venus <note>original: "Sita"</note> is joined by cruel planets, the result is similar. **2**

If Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note> is present, there may be separation. If Mars <note>original: "Bhauma"</note> is present, the woman is said to be of a reddish-fair complexion. If Mercury <note>original: "Budha"</note> is present, she has a slightly dark or wan complexion. If Saturn or Rahu <note>original: "Manda" or "Rahu"</note> are present, she is dark-complexioned and thin or bony. **3**

If the Sun <note>original: "Surya"</note> is present, the wife is said by the wise to be mature or aged <note>original: "kala-jirna"</note>. If Mercury or Venus <note>original: "Budha" or "Shukra"</note> are present, she is a young girl or in the full bloom of youth. If Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note> is present, she is a virtuous woman endowed with sons. If Saturn <note>original: "Manda"</note> is present, the wise say she is a hardworking or serving woman. **4**

If Jupiter and Saturn are present, she is said to be a diligent worker. If the Moon <note>original: "Chandra"</note> is present, one should understand her to have a truly feminine nature and intelligence. By other planets, those learned in the science of diseases and the body <term>roga-shastra-vid</term> should declare her nature to be more masculine. **5**

If the house is joined by gentle planets <term>saumya</term>, in a gentle sign, or aspected by a gentle planet, one should speak of a beautiful form and happiness for the woman. If mixed with false or cruel planets <note>malefics</note>, there is nothing of the sort in the house of the wife. **6**

In the place of the wife, if it is in a division of Venus and aspected by Venus, or in a division of a benefic, a male child is predicted. Similarly, if the Moon is there, or if Jupiter is present, it arises from having a single wife. **7**

If Venus is in a weakened state or aspected by two malefic planets, or even one, there will be a defect in the body of the man or a fear regarding his person. Through these house placements, one can also determine if the women of the house are pregnant. **8**

If Saturn or Ketu are present, or if they are associated with the lords of the house, there may be danger from poison, fire, or chronic diseases. If the Moon is present, she may suffer from constant headaches. With Ketu, she is never quite happy or is distressed in an instant. **9**

If Saturn and the Sun are in that place, or if they are in the signs of Gemini or Libra <note>original: "Yajne-mini-sine," likely referring to Mithuna and Tula</note>, the man's city <note>home</note> will be filled with women, or he may be burdened by them. He may be constantly troubled by "wind" diseases <term>vata-ruja</term> <note>Vata: one of the three humors in Ayurvedic medicine, associated with air, nerves, and movement</note> or have a very restless mind.

<meta>This page continues the analysis of the Seventh House, which governs marriage, the spouse, and partnerships. It transitions from general physical descriptions to specific astrological combinations that predict the spouse's character, health, and the longevity of the marriage. The text also correlates planetary positions with physical marks or birthmarks on the body.</meta>

One should also predict matters such as thieves and the like as taught by the great sages, as well as the presence of birthmarks <term>lanchana</term>. **10**

The wise and intelligent astrologers <term>daivachintaka</term> <note>literally "those who contemplate the divine/fate"</note> should consider that whatever results are found for the houses relating to one's own body, similar results will befall one's wife <note>original: "yoshita"</note>. **11**

If the Sun <note>original: "Arka"</note> is situated in a void <note>an empty house</note>, the person is governed by their previous nature; if there is a complication of the humors <note>original: "sannipata," a medical term for an imbalance of the three bodily humors</note>, the complexion is black <note>original: "krishna-varna"</note>, caused by an affliction of wind <note>original: "vayu," possibly referring to gout or respiratory issues</note>, excessive striking, or even by poison. **12**

Similarly, there may be dizziness, or what is known as a "wound of debt" <note>original: "rina-samjne vranena," perhaps signifying a persistent or recurring injury</note>, or minor diseases, intoxication, or contagion if malefic planets <note>original: "papa"</note> are positioned there. **13**

If Rahu is in a void house, injury may occur from animals, or by the strike of a weapon, or from a female spirit <note>original: "dakinya," referring to a malevolent supernatural being</note>, or through the consequences of one's own intense past deeds <note>karma</note>. If Ketu and the Moon <note>original: "Chandra"</note> are present, there are various disorders, perhaps from water or from "cold" diseases <note>original: "shishira-amaya"</note> such as wasting or consumption. **14**

The learned astrologers <term>horavid</term>, following the vision of the scriptures, should declare the results by understanding the placement of the Sun <note>original: "Khera"</note> in the house of relatives <note>the 4th house</note>, its neighboring houses, and the houses it aspects. **15**

If the Moon is the planet <term>kheta</term> sitting in a void house, then there is health and happiness of the limbs; likewise, if joined by benefic planets <note>original: "saumya"</note>, one born under the sign of Pisces <note>original: "mina-tanuja"</note> is said to be endowed with happiness. **16**

If a void house contains benefics, the man is thoughtful; if it contains cruel planets <note>original: "krura," meaning malefics</note>, he is said to be lazy and lustful. Through the influence of neuter planets, he becomes impotent <note>original: "klivatvam"</note>. If the Sun is in the house of the wife, and if she lives, she will be free from disease. **17**

If Rahu <note>original: "Sarbhanu"</note> is in that house, aspected by or joined with malefics, there will be no union with a wife; or if a union occurs, she dies after a short time. If joined with benefics, this union happens only after a long delay. **18**

Through the influence of the Sun and Moon, a man may have two wives, or one may be lost—this should be declared with certainty by the experts of birth charts <term>jatakajnai</term>. There may be a black birthmark <term>lanchana</term> at that location. **19**

If the Moon aspects that house, his wife will surely be fickle or restless. If Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note> is in that division, joined with or aspected by Mercury <note>original: "Budha"</note>, she will be focused on desire <note>original: "kamage"</note>. **20**

In the division of Saturn <note>original: "Saura-varga"</note>, if aspected by the "Son of the Waters" <note>original: "Varunaja," likely referring to the planet Mercury or a specific watery influence</note>, the couple <term>dampati</term> will be restless. If Venus <note>original: "Bhargava"</note> is there, his intellect is damaged and he becomes a pursuer of other men's wives. **21**

... [the text continues regarding the moon's divisions]

If there are malefic planets <term>papa-khedah</term> in the twelfth house from the Ascendant <term>lagna</term>, and the Moon is weak or situated in the fifth house, the man will be bereft of a wife <note>original: "patni-hino"</note> and bereft of sons <note>original: "putraihino"</note>; this should be understood through careful contemplation. **12** 

If the Sun <note>original: "Bhaskara"</note> and Mercury <note>original: "Indu-putra," literally 'son of the Moon'</note> are in the eighth <term>mrityu</term> or the sixth house, there is only one wife. Malefic planets calculated from the Ascendant or the Moon are destroyers of the spouse. For men, if the Moon is in the seventh house and Saturn <note>original: "Manda"</note> is also situated in the seventh, then the wife will certainly be of a sterile nature. **15** 

When Mars <note>original: "Bhumi-putra," literally 'son of the Earth'</note> occupies the Seventh House <term>dyuna-bhava</term>, the man will be constantly bereft of a beloved. Similarly, he is not pleased if it is aspected by Saturn. If aspected by benefic planets, this result does not occur. **16** 

If there is the defect of the "end-junction" <term>gandanta-dosha</term> <note>a spiritual knot or transitional point in the zodiac considered inauspicious</note>, and Venus <note>original: "Shukra"</note> is in the Seventh House while the Sun is in the fourth, then the man becomes the husband of a barren woman <note>original: "vandhya-piyah"</note>; this should be gathered from the summaries. **17** 

If the lords of the Seventh House are not aspected by Mercury or are not in the portions of the Sun, Mars, or Saturn, or are in an inauspicious interval... **18** 

When Mars <note>original: "Bhauma"</note> is in the house of enemies (the 6th) and Rahu <note>original: "Simhika-sutah," literally 'son of Simhika'</note> is in the seventh, the wife does not live, even if they have many children. **19** 

Now, concerning the combination for self-immolation <term>sahagamana-yoga</term> <note>the historical practice of a widow following her husband into the funeral pyre</note> as stated by the Light of Astrology <note>original: "Jyotirena," likely referring to a specific text or authority</note>: If the Lord of the Ninth <term>dharma-swami</term> is in the ninth house and the Sun is in the ascendant, she may enter the fire <note>original: "agni-pravesham"</note>. If the Lord of the Seventh <term>jamitra-natha</term> is otherwise situated, a different result is stated. **20**

Fear of enemies, loss of wealth, injury, or the number of those slain in times of battle, openings <note>vulnerabilities</note> in the world, and imprisonment—all this should be contemplated by the wise in the House of Wealth/Destruction <term>dravya-sthane</term>. **2** 

There, the primary factor is the strength of the house itself. Benefic planets give good results, while malefics give poor ones. Whatever results are said to arrive in the house of wealth/death, one must consider them based on the planetary defects. **3**

### Analysis of the Eighth House

A man is surely [afflicted] when benefic planets <note>original: "saumya," referring to gentle planets like Jupiter or Venus</note> are placed alongside malefic planets <note>original: "krura-graha," literally 'cruel planets' like Saturn or Mars</note> in this house; such a person will be distressed. **4** If the wicked planets are exalted <term>tungaih</term>, the person is born with wounds or scars; if a benefic is also there, the <unclear>mark</unclear> or wound will be on the left side of the body, which should be predicted by those wise in the science of birth <note>original: "jataka-jnaih," meaning astrologers</note>. **5** With malefics situated in the eighth house <term>ashtama-samsthitheshcha</term>, a person is plagued by disease; in battle, such a person is fond of strife, yet suffers a sudden defeat, though they may soon be released from captivity. **6**

When Rahu <note>the North Node of the Moon</note> is in the second house, one travels happily by boat; wealth should be predicted after carefully considering the Ascendant <term>vilagnam</term>. **6** <note>The numbering repeats in the source</note> If the Sun <note>original: "Arka"</note> is in the seventh house and joined by malefics, the person is not fit for enjoyments and is afflicted by diseases of the anus, the spleen, or the rectum. **7**

->Thus ends the brief analysis of the Eighth House.<-

### Analysis of the Ninth House

Regarding the <term>fruitful power</term> of the Ninth House for men: [one may look for] seven pilgrimages, a pure character, religious deeds, and the very ocean of fortune. **1** The construction of temples, ponds, and all such things which are called "meritorious acts" <note>original: "punya"</note> are found here in various ways; these results should be declared through the ninth house. **2**

If the Moon is joined by a benefic planet, all things will be beautiful; one will not be joined by diseases or afflicted by wounds on the left side, unless touched by malefics. **3** A man may have broken bones or be a "woman-made man" <note>possibly referring to effeminacy or social role</note> if Rahu is in the House of Dharma <note>the 9th house</note>. If the Sun is in an exalted sign, the person is not so greatly afflicted by these troubles; they will have sisters and possess happiness derived from the mother. **4**

A man with malefics here is sinful and stingy; there is no success in religious duties <term>dharma</term> when joined by wicked enemies. However, benefic planets situated there for men will always bring about the perfection of Dharma. **5** The ninth house from the Moon or the Ascendant is called the Abode of Fortune <term>bhagyalayam</term>. If the Lord of that house is present, the Lord of the Gods himself grants fortune; in this matter, I have seen the subjects of men. **6** Gentle planets <note>benefics</note> moving through the ninth house make one endowed with fortune; other planets <note>malefics</note> cause the attainment of sorrow in a foreign land. Fortune shines upon each man according to his own [planetary] nature. **7** The sixth and the...

If the Ninth House is devoid of two [specific strengths], the person may be of a violent nature, yet if Ketu and Mars are together there, they will always be possessed of wealth. **14**

### Analysis of the Tenth House

Regarding the <term>fruitful power</term> <note>original: "phala pravritti"</note> of the Tenth House: This is the place of the country, the two knees, the attainment of royalty, and <term>karma</term> <note>actions, profession, or destiny</note>. It also governs one's livelihood, trade, and the "seal" <note>original: "mudra," implying authority or official stamps</note> of travel and status. **1** 

A good residence, gains from the father, growth of one's own prosperity, the happiness of one's children, and royal commands <note>original: "nripa ajnam"</note>—all these should be carefully considered from the Tenth House and the arrival of wealth. **2** The results for the mother and father are determined by the lords of those respective houses and the planets transiting through them. <margin>19</margin> One should speak of the father's status through these planets. **3** 

If the Lord of the Tenth is a benefic planet endowed with strength, or if benefic planets are situated in that house, or if the planets moving through it are strong benefics, the results will be auspicious. **4** If the Moon <note>original: "shitaruchi," literally 'cold-rayed one'</note> is full and situated in the Fourth House, or if strong benefic planets are in the Tenth, one should predict good results arising from one's career and actions. **5** 

Happiness from the father and paternal uncles is certain [under these conditions]; however, if malefics are present, there may be distress related to the father or mother's family. **6** Jupiter <note>original: "vachaspatika"</note>...

**7** When Venus <note>original: "Shukra"</note> is in his own house <note>the signs of Taurus or Libra</note> and the full Moon <note>original: "Shashi"</note> is also present there, a person achieves the status of a ruler and receives many gifts and honors from other kings. **8** If the lords of the Twelfth and Fourth Houses are associated with benefic planets, it brings great happiness. However, if the lord of the house is associated with poverty and grief, the person will be afflicted by these sorrows.

**9** When planets associated with "gems among women" <note>likely referring to high-status marriages or noble connections</note> enter the House of Action <term>Karma Bhava</term>, the person is born into a royal lineage and possesses great wealth. If the Sun <note>original: "Arka"</note> is well-placed in the Tenth House and receives favorable aspects, the person's influence is unwavering. **10** Benefic planets situated in the Tenth House in their own signs, or receiving benefic aspects, ensure that the person will truly be a king within their own family and will be exceedingly generous. Alternatively, they may find great success in trade and commerce.

**11** If the Tenth House contains gentle planets <note>original: "saumya kheta," such as Mercury, Venus, or Jupiter</note> or is aspected by them, or if the Moon is similarly aspected without the interference of malefic planets, these royal results are confirmed. **12** If the Sun <note>original: "Vikarttana"</note> is in the Tenth House, the wise say the person may suffer from diseases or injuries to the upper body, or face wounds caused by weapons or fire.

**13** If Rahu <note>original: "Simhi-suta," the son of Simhika</note> and Saturn <note>original: "Surya-suta," the son of the Sun</note> are located there, the person will be pained by "windy" disorders <note>original: "samira dosha," referring to Vata imbalances in Ayurvedic medicine like joint pain or nervous issues</note>. The sages declare that this yoga <note>planetary combination</note> brings a blemish or suffering to the parents. **14** The person may lose their children or suffer greatly through them. There is also a danger of injury from stones, wood, falling from trees, or accidents involving water.

**15** When Saturn, the Sun, and Rahu are all in the Tenth House, and if Mars <note>original: "Bhauma"</note> aspects them, the person's father will travel to or live in a foreign land. **16** If favorable planets also influence this, the results are mitigated, but there remains a fear of the government <note>original: "rajatas," from the king</note> <margin>27</margin> or separation from the father.

**17** If the Sun <note>original: "Marttanda"</note> is in the Tenth House and is aspected by Saturn, the person may be abandoned by their father. **18** From the Tenth House <note>original: "Ambara," the sky/zenith</note>, one should determine a person's livelihood and profession. This is judged by the strength of the planets located there or the lord of the division. The person becomes famous through the results of their own planetary periods <term>dasha</term>.

<margin>19</margin> The daily activities and livelihood of a person are also influenced by the Moon. Through various efforts and great courage, they may acquire different types of wealth and enjoy great luxuries.

-> ### 20 ### <-

**20** When the Sun <note>original: "Dinakara," the day-maker</note> is positioned in the tenth house <note>the "sky" or highest point in a chart</note> from either the Ascendant or the Moon, the individual acquires wealth through various occupations. Due to this planetary alignment, they may achieve leadership over others, or even the status of a ruler of men, provided they act with a gracious mind.

**21** If Mars <note>original: "Mahija," the son of the Earth</note> is in the tenth house from the Ascendant or the Moon and is associated with "cruel" <term>malefic</term> planets, the person’s livelihood will involve hardship. Such a man may have a mind inclined toward violence or may earn his living through the use of weapons and physical prowess.

**22** When Mercury <note>original: "Banashija"</note> occupies the tenth house from the Ascendant or the Moon, it brings forth wealth and leadership. This success is achieved through noble courage, the mastery of poetic arts, and various forms of craftsmanship or technical skill.

**23** If Jupiter <note>original: "Guru"</note> is situated in the tenth house from the Ascendant, it brings an influx of diverse kinds of wealth. Such a man receives great honor from the protectors of the earth <note>kings or government officials</note> and maintains a noble livelihood through his financial dealings.

**24** When Venus <note>original: "Bhrigusuta," the son of Bhrigu</note> is in the tenth house from the Ascendant or the Moon, the person becomes a master of all sciences and arts. Their mind is inclined toward charity and humility; they possess a virtuous character and enjoy fame, pleasures, and high honor from the ruler of the land.

**25** Saturn <note>original: "Shanaishchara," the slow-mover</note> in the tenth house, when lacking the strength of the Moon and associated with other planets, creates a livelihood marked by meanness. This position brings physical misery, poverty, and constant anxiety or grief, despite the person's attempts to maintain a good character.

**26** One should determine the specific results of the Sun and other planets when they are in the tenth house or the tenth divisional chart <term>Dashamansha</term> during their respective planetary periods <term>Dasha</term>. From these, one can discern if wealth comes from the father, the mother, an enemy, a sibling, or from the spouse’s family.

**27** The specific nature of one's profession should be declared based on the Sun, the Moon, or the Ascendant. The occupation will be shaped by the qualities of the planets that exert the most influence. **28** The Moon <note>original: "Shitagu," the cool-rayed one</note> governs livelihoods related to agriculture, water, or liquids. If malefic planets are involved, the person may earn through daring but violent acts or the use of weapons.

**29** Jupiter <note>original: "Surarchitah," the one worshiped by the gods</note> grants a livelihood through the study of scriptures, poetic vision, and sacred arts, providing the person with a leading role among the learned. **30** Venus <note>original: "Kavi," the poet</note> indicates a profession related to women, fine silks, or luxuries. However, if the planets are positioned in very lowly or weakened states, the livelihood may be of a base nature, even if the person possesses some inherent strength of fortune.

**31** Whichever planet rules the tenth house, if it is joined with powerful benefic planets and situated in a house of gain, it ensures that the man will possess a great treasury, land, and widespread fame.

**Thus concludes the consideration of the Tenth House <term>Karma Bhava</term>.**

-> ### The Fruits of the Eleventh House (House of Gains) ### <-

**1** All that has been explained by the great experts of the *Harika* scriptures <note>likely referring to a specific lineage of astrological tradition</note>, beginning with the god Shiva and other sages, must be applied here. **2** One should carefully contemplate the "House of Gains" <note>original: *labhalaya*</note> to determine the acquisition of knowledge, wealth, gold, elephants, horses, palanquins, chariots, fine garments, and auspicious ornaments. 

**3** If the "lamps" <note>a poetic term for planets</note> are all well-placed in auspicious signs and associated with gentle <term>benefic</term> planets, or even if "cruel" <term>malefic</term> planets are placed in the third, sixth, or eleventh houses <note>the *Upachaya* or houses of growth where even difficult planets produce good results</note>, the man becomes endowed with great fortune. 

**4** If the Son of the Sun <note>original: *Arkatanaya*, meaning Saturn</note> is in the House of Gains, the person may possess wealth as vast as the ears of an elephant. If the Sun <note>original: *Tapana*, the burner</note> is there, he possesses horses. If the Son of the Moon <note>original: *Vidhusuta*, meaning Mercury</note> is there, he enjoys comfortable beds, and if Venus <note>original: *Bhriguja*, the descendant of Bhrigu</note> is present, he acquires many vehicles.

**5** When the Moon <note>original: *Himagu*, the cool-rayed one</note> and the Counselor of the King of Gods <note>original: *Surendra-sachiva*, meaning Jupiter</note> are in the eleventh house, the man enjoys the happiness of fine clothes and a beautiful wife. He becomes a man of learning and great wealth, honored even by the "King of Kings." If Mercury and Venus are there with the Moon, he may have many daughters; if Jupiter is also present there, he remains learned but may have a slender physique.

**6** All the houses are said to be auspicious when the Lord of the Eleventh is strong. Whatever results are produced by the planets in sacrifices and religious acts, they are truly seen here. **7** When Rahu <note>the North Node of the Moon</note> is positioned there, the man is full of majesty and valor, possesses wealth and sons, and is respected by kings. If Saturn is there, he may have few children but will live a very long life.

**8** If Venus is afflicted <note>original: *bhanga*, broken/weakened</note> in three ways, the man may suffer an injury to his arm or side. If Mars <note>original: *Bhauma*</note> is in the eleventh house, the man may suffer from the effects of poison, fire, or harsh speech, and find himself constantly troubled. 

**9** If the Sun and Mercury are in the eleventh house along with Venus and Jupiter, and the planet governing women <note>the Moon</note> is also strong, many sons will live. Otherwise, the man may find little happiness from his progeny. 

**10** With Rahu and Saturn together, the man may be one-eyed <note>original: *kana*</note> or suffer from injuries in childhood. If this combination is aspected <note>viewed</note> by the Lord of the Ascendant, he might be harmed by a dog <note>original: *kukkurena*</note> in his youth. 

**11** When the Lord of the Eleventh is associated with benefics, the person gains wealth through kings. If the Moon is in the eleventh house and situated in its own division, the person gains wealth through women, elephants, and horses, becoming "full" <note>complete or wealthy</note> like the waxing moon.

<summary>This page details the astrological effects of various planets when located in the Eleventh House (House of Gains), predicting wealth, vehicles, family lineage, and specific physical ailments.</summary>

</margin>

...wealth is gained through friends, power, weapons, and journeys. If <term>benefic</term> <note>gentle or auspicious</note> planets are present, the arrival of wealth is indicated, and the person may delight in ornaments for the left eye <note>The 11th and 12th houses are often associated with the left side of the body, particularly the ears and eyes.</note>. Through Mercury <note>original: *Saumya*</note>, one acquires a collection of arts and skills; such a person protects their livelihood through writing and accumulates wealth in this world through the influence of women and respectful, diligent efforts. **12** 

Through Jupiter <note>original: *Jiva*</note>, the human is honored, possesses vehicles, and is favored by the king. He may find his livelihood through "black grains" <note>likely referring to specific agricultural crops like black gram or sesame</note> or other life-sustaining means. Through Venus <note>original: *Daitya-guru*</note>, wealth arrives through women and those skilled in the arts. Through Saturn <note>original: *Manda*</note>, gains come from blue elephants, iron, buffaloes, and auspicious victories in battle. **13** 

If the Eleventh House is viewed or joined by planets in their highest dignity, the person attains great enjoyment and wealth. When all the "graspers" <note>original: *Kheta*, a term for the planets</note> are situated in or aspect this house, such a great abundance of gains is experienced by men. **14** 

**Thus ends the consideration of the House of Gains.**

I shall now speak of the results of the **House of Losses** <note>original: *Vyayasthana*, the 12th house</note>. For men, this house represents the pair of feet. Loss, fines, imprisonment, and many types of expenditures are determined by the planets residing here. **1** In the House of Losses, one must carefully consider efforts regarding water reservoirs and various other public works. **2** As are the "sky-goers" <note>original: *Gaganaga*, another term for planets</note> situated in the twelfth house, so shall the person's expenditures always be. Whatever is earned by the person goes to the king's palace <note>meaning taxes or legal fines</note> if certain planets are in this house. **3** 

If a planet associated with phlegm <note>original: *Shleshma*, one of the three bodily humors or 'doshas' in Ayurvedic medicine</note> is in the House of Losses, wealth is spent on sons, or given to others, or spent on the left ear or eye. **4** If <term>malefic</term> <note>harsh or cruel</note> planets are dominant, there may be a loss of life-force or wealth; the body may suffer from burns by fire, or long-term ailments such as respiratory blockages and sores. **5** 

If Mercury <note>original: *Budha*</note> is present, wealth is lost through expenditure on women, or for the delight of the people through music and chanting related to phlegmatic diseases. **6** If Saturn <note>original: *Manda*</note> is in the house of expenditure at the time of birth, wealth is consumed by phlegmatic illnesses, or there is a loss caused by sons or enemies. **7** 

If the Moon <note>original: *Chandra*</note> is present, wealth goes toward water-related ventures. If joined by the Sun <note>original: *Arka*</note>, loss occurs through the government or those in power. If joined by Venus <note>original: *Chandraja*, often used for Mercury, but context suggests Venusian/feminine influences</note>, it is spent on fine women or noble groups. **8** If associated with iron, wood, or stones, loss occurs through those materials, or through weapons such as the *Bhindipala* <note>a traditional Indian sling or javelin-like weapon</note> or through horned animals.

<meta>This page continues the astrological discourse on planetary placements within the twelve houses of a horoscope. Having concluded the House of Gains (11th house) on the previous page, this folio focuses on the Twelfth House—the house of loss, expenditure, and the afterlife—before summarizing the entire system and beginning a technical discussion on the Ascendant.</meta>

->44<-

If one is constantly tormented by ailments related to water, or suffers from diseases as if chronically ill, it is surely because the Sun <note>original: *Ravi*</note> is situated in the <term>Twelfth House</term> <note>original: *Vyaya-bhava*, the house of expenditure and loss</note> of a man's chart. In such a state, he does not find the favor of his father, nor is he accompanied by the virtuous. **9-10**

If Venus, Mercury, and Jupiter <note>original: *Shukra, Jna, Jiva*</note> are situated in the House of Loss, they may act as helpers to the father, yet the person may suffer the loss of a spouse or a shortened lifespan. If Rahu <note>original: *Tamah*, literally 'darkness'</note> is joined with Venus in the twelfth, a time of conflict or fruitlessness arrives for him. **11**

If <unclear>Rahu/Ketu</unclear> <note>original: *Rahu-mipa*</note> is stationed in the twelfth, strength and vigor are lost. If Mars <note>original: *Bhauma*</note> is also joined there, his power is further diminished. **12**

A full Moon or Venus in the twelfth house causes the destruction of one's entire fortune. If the Moon is in the twelfth and is aspected by or joined with Mercury <note>original: *Chandra-ja*, 'son of the moon'</note> or Mars <note>original: *Bhu-suta*, 'son of the earth'</note>, there will be a continuous loss of wealth. **13**

If Jupiter <note>original: *Dhishana*</note> is in the twelfth, he makes one a giver but one who enjoys the labor of others. Saturn <note>original: *Manda*</note> in this position makes one a builder of water-cisterns, wells, and ponds; if Mars <note>original: *Saro-ma-ja*</note> is there, the person becomes a practitioner of agriculture. **14**

[The planets] grant abundant gold, beautiful clothes, cattle-wealth, and horses. If Venus <note>original: *Sita*</note> and the Moon <note>original: *Shitaru*</note> are favorable, and if joined by Mercury <note>original: *Budha*</note>, the person possesses vast lands. **15**

Mercury <note>original: *Shashija*</note> combined with the Sun <note>original: *Ravi*</note> grants a life of service along with ornaments of gems and gold. If Jupiter <note>original: *Jiva*</note> is with Venus <note>original: *Sita*</note>, the person is wealthy, a performer of good deeds, and is filled with devotion, constantly meditating on <term>Hari</term> <note>A name for Lord Vishnu, indicating a pious or devotional nature</note>. **16**

Benefic planets in the <term>kendras</term> <note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th houses, considered the 'pillars' of a chart</note> or in the twelfth house definitely bring good results at the time of birth. Indeed, all benefics in the twelfth with the Sun produce [wealth], yet they do not eliminate the confusion caused by enemies. **17**

Malefic planets <note>original: *Krura*, 'cruel ones'</note> stationed in the twelfth, even if powerful, cause confusion among enemies but later lead to regret throughout the years or at the time of birth. **18**

Thus, the entire results arising from the twelve houses have been told by Lord <term>Shambhu</term> <note>Shiva</note> for the sake of <term>Aruna</term> <note>The charioteer of the Sun</note> and to <term>Vasishtha</term> and other great sages for their own disciples. **19**

**Thus ends the consideration of the House of Loss.**

The ancient sages say: Regarding the <term>Lagna</term> <note>the Ascendant or 1st House</note>, planets in the first half of the sign do not [immediately] grant their fruits; the results are given in the latter half of the duration. Others say the results are seen upon the approach of the latter half. **1**

One should observe the Ascendant: if the Lord of the Ascendant, or the Lord of the Action <note>the planet governing the specific matter being queried</note>, aspects the House of Action, or if the Lord of the Ascendant aspects the House of Action...

# Concerning the Success of Undertakings

If the Lord of the Undertaking <term>Karyadhipa</term> <note>The planet ruling the house associated with the specific goal or question</note> is in the degree of the Ascendant, or if the Lord of the Ascendant <term>Lagnadhipati</term> is situated in the House of the Undertaking and aspects <note>original: *prapashyet*, meaning to cast an astrological gaze or aspect upon</note> the Lord of the Undertaking, or if the Lord of the Undertaking is in the Ascendant and aspects the Lord of the Ascendant, the task will reach **success**. **2** 

If the Lord of the Undertaking aspects the Lord of the Ascendant, or if the Lord of the Ascendant is in the House of the Undertaking, and in all these cases they receive the aspect of the "Nectar-Rayed One" <note>original: *Piyusha-mayukha*, a poetic name for the Moon</note>, then the success of that task is assured at that **time**. **4** 

Or, if the Lord of the Ascendant is in the Ascendant and the Lord of the Undertaking is in the House of the Undertaking, or if the Lord of the Undertaking is placed in the Ascendant, you shall obtain the **result**. **5** 

If the Lord of the Ascendant and the Lord of the Undertaking are both in the Ascendant, or both in the House of the Undertaking, and are aspected by the "King of the Twice-Born" <note>original: *Dvija-raja*, referring to the Moon</note>, they bring about the success of the task at that time; this occurs when the Lord of the Ascendant and the Lord of the Undertaking are aspected by the **Ascendant**. **6** 

Whether in a birth chart <term>Prasuti</term> or a question chart <term>Prashna</term>, if the Lord of the Ascendant and the Lord of the Undertaking are in the same sign <note>original: *eka-golau*</note>, or in the same division or sub-division <term>Navamsha</term>, the outcome should be considered. First, there may be mixed results, and then the final **fruit**. 

->Thus ends the consideration of Success in Undertakings.<-

In the *Vidvaj-jna-meru-kheta-lila-vilasa*, in the essence of the *Shambhu Hora Prakasha* <note>The "Light of Shambhu's Astrology"</note> as proclaimed by Pujaraja, the **Sixth Section**, which is the **Chapter on the Twelve Houses**, is **complete**. **251** <margin>2</margin>

# Longevity and the Eight-fold Division

If the Moon is in a Cancer Ascendant <note>original: *Karki-dvija-yute lagne*</note>, and Mercury and Venus <note>original: *Saumya Bhargavau*</note> are in the angles <term>Kendra</term> or aspecting them, the person may reach a lifespan of one hundred years, appearing like the Lord of Sindura <note>original: *Sindureshwara*, likely a reference to a long-lived deity or Ganesha</note>. **1** 

Lifespan is determined by four methods: the Pinda, the Nisarga, the Jiva, and the fourth being the Amsa <note>Technical methods of calculating longevity based on planetary degrees and nature</note>. The fifth is described hereafter, and the sixth is said to be the results of the Eight-fold Division <term>Ashtakavarga</term>. This has been declared by the ancients as a "sea of age." **2**

Regarding short life, the factors are calculated by technical proportions <note>original: *kirana*, rays or units of measurement</note>. The calculations involve factors of three, or three and a half. There, one must look at the multipliers for the various planetary strengths. **3** 

The balance of their paths is calculated; whichever planet is in its exaltation, its own division, the "Nectar" division, or a friend's division is considered equal; but it is a "foe" if in an enemy's division. **4**

If the Lord of the division is in an enemy's house, the values are: eleven (11), fifteen (15), thirteen (13), nine (9), five (5), five (5), and three (3). These are placed for the planets and doubled. **5** 

These combinations are then divided in sequence by the numerical values of the senses (5), the directions (8), or the mountains (7) <note>This uses *Bhuta-sankhya*, a system where Sanskrit words represent numbers</note>. By multiplying by six and using the divisions of the signs, the strength of the planets is determined. If a planet is in a friend's house or a "Gautama" position <note>likely a specific technical placement</note>, the multiplier is fifty-three (53). After obtaining the result...

# The Calculation of Longevity (Ayurdaya)

The calculations for the even and odd signs should be performed separately. **7** The planetary strengths are determined by the oceans <note>original: *sagara*, a numerical code for 4</note> and the sky <note>original: *nabha*, a numerical code for 0</note>. For the Sun and the other planets <note>original: *ravipurvakanam*, starting with the Sun</note>, one must multiply the clarified planetary positions to find the appropriate multipliers for the work of determining life-span. **8**

The degrees <note>original: *ansha*</note> of the planets <term>Kheta</term> and the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term> must be observed. When the Ascendant has traveled six signs, one must calculate the minutes of arc <note>original: *kala*</note> to find the precise planetary portion. **9** One should calculate the full "Rama" <note>original: *purna-rama*, a numerical code for 30</note> for the benefics <note>original: *saumya*</note> and others. **10**

### The Solar Longevity System

Subtract the longitude of the planet from the longitude of its exaltation point. The resulting degrees and minutes are then multiplied by the specific factors for each planet. **11** These portions of the period <term>Dayansha</term> are then divided by the "complete-complete-fire" <note>original: *purna-purna-agni*, a numerical code for 300</note> to arrive at the years, months, and days of life. **12**

The years calculated from these portions are further refined by the planetary strengths. This method is known by the learned sages as the **Solar Longevity** <note>original: *Saura Ayurdaya*</note> because the calculation is based on the movement of the Sun. **13**

### Adjustments for Planetary Motion

Except in cases of retrograde motion <term>Vakragati</term> <note>When a planet appears to move backward in the sky from the perspective of Earth</note>, the calculated years must be reduced if a planet is in its sign of debilitation or in an enemy’s house. **14** If the planet is situated in an unfriendly sign, its contribution to the lifespan is halved; however, if the planet is in its own sign or exalted, no such reduction is made. This is the method described by the ancients for the Natural <term>Nisarga</term> system. **15**

The portions of the life-period <note>original: *Dayansha*</note> should be placed in separate columns for each planet and the Ascendant. **16** One must then account for the "gaze" or aspect <note>original: *drishti*</note> of both benefics and malefics <note>original: *krura*</note>, and whether the planets are in "ascending" or "descending" houses. **17** If a planet is in its own "abode" or is aspected by its friend, its strength increases; if it is in an unfavorable position, the fruit of its calculation is diminished. **18**

# Observations on the Ascendant Lord

If the Lord of the Ascendant <term>Lagnapa</term> is himself situated in the Ascendant, even if he is a malefic <note>original: *krura*</note>, he is not considered a destroyer of the life-span. The previous masters of this science have stated that such a position actually protects the "valour" and longevity of the individual. **19**

### The Natural Lifespan Values

In the Natural system, specific years are assigned to each planet:
*   The Sun: 20 years
*   The Moon: 1 year
*   Mars: 2 years
*   Mercury: 9 years
*   Jupiter: 18 years
*   Venus: 20 years
*   Saturn: 50 years <note>These represent the "Natural" (Nisarga) years which are then modified by the planet's actual position in the birth chart.</note> **20**

The period lords <note>original: *dayesha*</note> are then multiplied by their respective qualities to find the final determination.

# The Fourfold Determination of Longevity

From the planetary divisions, one obtains the years and other time units. This applies to the **Pinda** <note>original: *painda*, a system based on the total positions of planets</note> and **Naisargika** <note>original: *naisargika*, the natural or inherent lifespan assigned to each planet</note> systems in order. **21** If Jupiter and the Moon are in the Ascendant, the lifespan is increased. One should multiply the years by the appropriate fractions and divide to find the refined result. **22**

The years and minutes of arc <note>original: *lipta*</note> are determined by the lord of the division. In the **Pinda** system, the longevity is equal to the total degrees of the Ascendant <term>Lagna</term>. **23** According to some authorities, when a planet is in its own house or exaltation, its strength is considered supreme, and the longevity is calculated accordingly through the degrees. **24**

The ancients have spoken of four types of longevity calculations, which are proclaimed here. **25** The system of **Degree-Longevity** <term>Amsayu</term> is used when the Ascendant is strong; **Pinda-Longevity** is used when the Sun is strong; and **Natural-Longevity** <note>original: *naisargika*</note> is used when the Moon is strong. If all three are endowed with strength, the results are combined. **26**

If the fire-like planets <note>referring to the Sun and Mars</note> are strong, one should multiply by their strength and divide by the sum. If the planets lack strength, the calculated lifespan is reduced. **27** This calculation of longevity is in accordance with the teachings of the sage **Satyacharya**. One must account for the influence of benefics <term>Shubha</term> and malefics <term>Papina</term>. **28**

Loss of years is said to occur in proportion to the malefic influence, while a small increase occurs due to the presence of benefics. **29** The calculation of life applies not only to humans but also to creatures like elephants, horses, and lions <note>referring to the various signs of the Zodiac or perhaps literal animal husbandry</note>. **30** When the calculation of the maximum lifespan is divided and adjusted, the precise <note>original: *sphuta*</note> longevity becomes clear. **31**

### Conclusion of the Fourfold Means

-> Thus concludes the **Fourfold Means of Determining Longevity** <-

By calculating the degrees and minutes from the Ascendant and the planets, and by dividing the remainders, one arrives at the years, months, and days of life. **32** If the Ascendant <term>Horā</term> is strong, the results are derived from the signs. The remaining portions are multiplied by two and added to the previous months. **33** By following these three methods of calculation and dividing by twelve, the remainder reveals the years, months, and days of the lifespan, as declared by the expert calculators. **34** One must also consider the positions of planets in their fall <note>debilitation</note>, exaltation, or enemy houses to adjust the final results.

...the portions spoken of for the third and fourth quarters are reduced. If a planet is in its own sign, the auspicious results are halved (35). If it is located in a single place, the Lord of the Yoga <note>A specific planetary combination</note> provides immediate results.

When a planet is endowed with strength, or is in its own **Vargottama** <note>original: *vargottama*, a position where a planet occupies the same zodiac sign in both the main chart and the ninth harmonic chart, increasing its power</note>, the value is doubled. # The Eight-Fold Grid
### (Ashtakavarga)

Now I shall proclaim the longevity born of the **Eight-Fold Grid** <term>Ashtakavarga</term> according to the method of **Maya** <note>A legendary figure in Indian astronomy/astrology</note>. I shall speak of the specific method known as "Individual Longevity" <note>original: *bhinnayu*</note>, as established in the traditional knowledge of the twice-born (1). One should indicate the placements arising from the planets in their respective order, following the mnemonic phrases <note>original: *vakyas*</note> of the Eight-Fold Grid (2). 

One should write these upon the wheel of the zodiac signs <term>Rashi-chakra</term>, combining the numbers accordingly. One should then determine the eleventh house in the proper sequence (3). 

**The Numerical Mnemonic Code:**

He describes the phrases for the Sun’s years in the order of the verses: From the **Sun** <note>original: *Arka*</note>, the positions are [represented by the code phrases]... from **Mars** <note>original: *Kuja*</note>... from **Venus** <note>original: *Bhargava*</note>... from **Mercury** <note>original: *Budha*</note>... from **Jupiter** <note>original: *Suraguru*</note>... and from the **Ascendant** <term>Lagna</term>. These numbers represent the auspicious points <note>original: *bindus*</note> (4).

Thus are the results for the Sun.

**The Grid of Mars:**
Similarly for Mars <note>original: *Vakra*</note>, the phrases concerning the Sun and the Sun’s son <note>Saturn</note>... the positions for Mercury and Venus... and the multipliers for the Sun.

### The Eight-fold Chart of the Moon
<term>Ashtakavarga (Eight-fold Chart): A system where planets gain points (bindus) based on their position relative to the seven planets and the Ascendant.</term>

The previous dwelling, the place of assembly, is proclaimed as the gathering. From the Sun: the songs, the son of the Sun <note>referring to Saturn</note>, from Mars: the sky, the cows, the female. From the Moon: the three sons. From Mercury: the remainder of the wealthy, well-worshipped. Similarly, the auspicious necklace from the Sun and others. Regarding the position of the womb, from the Sun’s eight-fold chart: 3. The eight-fold chart of the Sun is learned. From the Moon’s own eight-fold chart, the sons are in the field. From the wealth of Ganesha at the end, the Moon is a giver of auspiciousness through disease <note>This likely refers to specific technical mnemonic syllables like "ru-ja"</note>. The disciplined yogi, the good person, the one of good intelligence—the result is fruitless. The previous dwelling place of Jupiter is proclaimed as the field. The purification of the womb, and from Venus, the Moon is proclaimed. For the son, Saturn and Mercury are the auspicious ones. The songs and the movement of the Ascendant. 
**This is the Moon’s Eight-fold Chart.** <note>original: *iti candrāṣṭaka vargaḥ*</note>

### The Eight-fold Chart of Mars

Mars is a giver of auspiciousness. From the Sun, the son of the smoke is situated. From the Moon, the son and the master, Mars, is the vessel of strength for the leader. For the peace of cows, from Jupiter, it is auspicious. From the brilliance of Venus and Saturn, the poet <note>Venus</note> is the one to be established in the stars. From that constant expansion, the position of Mars is a giver of auspiciousness.
**This is the Mars’ Eight-fold Chart.** <note>original: *iti bhaumāṣṭakavargaḥ*</note>

### The Eight-fold Chart of Mercury

Mercury is placed at the beginning. From the Moon, the giver of wealth. From the previous dwelling, Mercury is the lord of the earth from Mars. The yoga of the vessel of the field, and Mercury is the position of Jupiter. The son, the womb, the great wealth; Mercury and Venus are givers of auspiciousness. From the previous dwelling, the sons of the Sun are givers of auspiciousness. Even from the Moon, Mercury is the highest giver of auspiciousness. 
**This is the Mercury’s Eight-fold Chart.** <note>original: *iti budhāṣṭaka vargaḥ*</note>

The profit of travel is proclaimed from the Sun and Jupiter. The village is established by Saturn...
